📄 src/App.tsx 1220 lines 
/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { User, Chat, Story, CallState, Dialect, Message, FollowRequest, AdminPermissions, AppNotification } from './types/chat';
import { DEFAULT_ADMIN_USER } from './data/mockData';
import { AuthScreen } from './components/AuthScreen';
import { ChatList } from './components/ChatList';
import { ChatConversation } from './components/ChatConversation';
import { StoryViewer } from './components/StoryViewer';
import { CallScreen } from './components/CallScreen';
import { NewChatModal } from './components/NewChatModal';
import { SettingsModal } from './components/SettingsModal';
import { AdminPanelModal } from './components/AdminPanelModal';
import { NotificationsModal } from './components/NotificationsModal';
import { CoinWalletModal } from './components/CoinWalletModal';
import { NotificationToast } from './components/NotificationToast';
import { VoiceChatModal } from './components/VoiceChatModal';
import { DominoLiveView } from './components/DominoLiveView';
import { BottomNavBar, ActiveTab } from './components/BottomNavBar';
import { FriendsExploreView } from './components/FriendsExploreView';
import { StoriesFeedView } from './components/StoriesFeedView';
import { playReceiveSound } from './utils/audio';
import { Smartphone, Monitor, Bell, X, MessageSquare, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { KurdistanSunEmoji } from './components/KurdistanBadge';

export default function App() {
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('kurd_chat_user');
      return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });

  const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([]);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [stories, setStories] = useState<Story[]>([]);
  const [activeChatPartners, setActiveChatPartners] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('kurd_chat_partners');
      return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  });

  const [selectedChatId, setSelectedChatId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeNavTab, setActiveNavTab] = useState<ActiveTab>('home');
  const [followRequests, setFollowRequests] = useState<FollowRequest[]>([]);
  const [dialect, setDialect] = useState<Dialect>('badini');
  const [soundEnabled, setSoundEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'mobile' | 'responsive'>('mobile');

  // Broadcast announcement banner
  const [adminAnnouncement, setAdminAnnouncement] = useState<{
    text: string;
    adminName: string;
    timestamp: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Modals & Overlays
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<AppNotification[]>([]);
  const [activeToast, setActiveToast] = useState<AppNotification | null>(null);
  const [isNotificationsOpen, setIsNotificationsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeStoryIndex, setActiveStoryIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [callState, setCallState] = useState<CallState | null>(null);
  const [isVoiceChatOpen, setIsVoiceChatOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isVoiceChatMinimized, setIsVoiceChatMinimized] = useState(false);
  const [voiceChatContact, setVoiceChatContact] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [isNewChatOpen, setIsNewChatOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isSettingsOpen, setIsSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isAdminOpen, setIsAdminOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isWalletOpen, setIsWalletOpen] = useState(false);
  const [bannedUsers, setBannedUsers] = useState<string[]>([]);

  // Persist current user & chat partners
  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentUser) {
      localStorage.setItem('kurd_chat_user', JSON.stringify(currentUser));
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('kurd_chat_user');
    }
  }, [currentUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('kurd_chat_partners', JSON.stringify(activeChatPartners));
  }, [activeChatPartners]);

  // Initial Fetch from backend
  const fetchInitialData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [usersRes, messagesRes, storiesRes, statsRes, requestsRes, notifsRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch('/api/users').catch(() => null),
        fetch('/api/messages').catch(() => null),
        fetch('/api/stories').catch(() => null),
        fetch('/api/admin/stats').catch(() => null),
        fetch('/api/friends/requests').catch(() => null),
        currentUser ? fetch(`/api/notifications?userId=${currentUser.id}`).catch(() => null) : null,
      ]);

      if (usersRes && usersRes.ok) {
        const uData = await usersRes.json();
        if (Array.isArray(uData.users)) setUsers(uData.users);
      }

      if (messagesRes && messagesRes.ok) {
        const mData = await messagesRes.json();
        if (Array.isArray(mData.messages)) setMessages(mData.messages);
      }

      if (storiesRes && storiesRes.ok) {
        const sData = await storiesRes.json();
        if (Array.isArray(sData.stories)) setStories(sData.stories);
      }

      if (statsRes && statsRes.ok) {
        const statData = await statsRes.json();
        if (statData.bannedUsers) setBannedUsers(statData.bannedUsers);
      }

      if (requestsRes && requestsRes.ok) {
        const rData = await requestsRes.json();
        if (Array.isArray(rData.requests)) setFollowRequests(rData.requests);
      }

      if (notifsRes && notifsRes.ok) {
        const nData = await notifsRes.json();
        if (Array.isArray(nData.notifications)) setNotifications(nData.notifications);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error fetching initial backend data:', err);
    }
  }, [currentUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchInitialData();
  }, [fetchInitialData]);

  // SSE Stream for live real-time network sync
  useEffect(() => {
    let eventSource: EventSource | null = null;
    try {
      eventSource = new EventSource('/api/stream');

      eventSource.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
          if (data.type === 'NEW_MESSAGE') {
            const msg: Message = data.payload;
            setMessages((prev) => (prev.some((m) => m.id === msg.id) ? prev : [...prev, msg]));

            if (currentUser && msg.senderId !== currentUser.id && soundEnabled) {
              playReceiveSound();
            }
          } else if (data.type === 'NEW_STORY') {
            const story: Story = data.payload;
            setStories((prev) => (prev.some((s) => s.id === story.id) ? prev : [story, ...prev]));
          } else if (data.type === 'USER_REGISTERED') {
            const newUser: User = data.payload;
            setUsers((prev) => (prev.some((u) => u.id === newUser.id) ? prev : [...prev, newUser]));
          } else if (data.type === 'USER_UPDATED') {
            const updatedUser: User = data.payload;
            setUsers((prev) => prev.map((u) => (u.id === updatedUser.id ? updatedUser : u)));
            setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev && prev.id === updatedUser.id ? { ...prev, ...updatedUser } : prev));
          } else if (data.type === 'ADMIN_BROADCAST') {
            setAdminAnnouncement(data.payload);
          } else if (data.type === 'CLEAR_CHATS') {
            setMessages([]);
          } else if (data.type === 'ADMIN_NAME_UPDATED') {
            setUsers((prev) =>
              prev.map((u) => (u.username === 'sabah_sndi' ? { ...u, name: data.payload.name } : u))
            );
          } else if (data.type === 'USER_BANNED_STATE') {
            const { username, isBanned } = data.payload;
            setBannedUsers((prev) =>
              isBanned ? [...prev.filter((u) => u !== username), username] : prev.filter((u) => u !== username)
            );
          } else if (data.type === 'USER_DELETED') {
            const { userId } = data.payload;
            setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== userId));
            setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.senderId !== userId));
            setStories((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.userId !== userId));
          } else if (data.type === 'MESSAGE_DELETED') {
            const { messageId } = data.payload;
            setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== messageId));
          } else if (data.type === 'CONVERSATION_DELETED') {
            const { chatId } = data.payload;
            setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => (m as { chatId?: string }).chatId !== chatId));
          } else if (data.type === 'STORY_DELETED') {
            const { storyId } = data.payload;
            setStories((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== storyId));
          } else if (data.type === 'FOLLOW_REQUEST_SENT') {
            const req: FollowRequest = data.payload;
            setFollowRequests((prev) =>
              prev.some((r) => r.id === req.id)
                ? prev.map((r) => (r.id === req.id ? req : r))
                : [...prev, req]
            );
          } else if (data.type === 'FOLLOW_REQUEST_UPDATED') {
            const req: FollowRequest = data.payload;
            setFollowRequests((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === req.id ? req : r)));
          } else if (data.type === 'FOLLOW_REQUEST_REMOVED') {
            const { fromUserId, toUserId } = data.payload;
            setFollowRequests((prev) =>
              prev.filter(
                (r) => !(r.fromUserId === fromUserId && r.toUserId === toUserId)
              )
            );
          } else if (data.type === 'NEW_NOTIFICATION') {
            const notif: AppNotification = data.payload;
            if (currentUser && notif.userId === currentUser.id) {
              setNotifications((prev) => (prev.some((n) => n.id === notif.id) ? prev : [notif, ...prev]));
              setActiveToast(notif);
              if (soundEnabled) playReceiveSound();
            }
          } else if (data.type === 'NOTIFICATIONS_READ') {
            const { userId, notificationId } = data.payload;
            if (currentUser && userId === currentUser.id) {
              setNotifications((prev) =>
                prev.map((n) => (!notificationId || n.id === notificationId ? { ...n, read: true } : n))
              );
            }
          } else if (data.type === 'NOTIFICATIONS_CLEARED') {
            const { userId } = data.payload;
            if (currentUser && userId === currentUser.id) {
              setNotifications([]);
            }
          } else if (data.type === 'STORY_LIKE_UPDATED') {
            const { storyId, likesCount, likedBy } = data.payload;
            setStories((prev) =>
              prev.map((s) => (s.id === storyId ? { ...s, likesCount, likedBy } : s))
            );
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Error parsing SSE event:', e);
        }
      };

      eventSource.onerror = () => {
        // SSE reconnects automatically
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('EventSource connection error:', err);
    }

    return () => {
      if (eventSource) eventSource.close();
    };
  }, [currentUser, soundEnabled]);

  // Compute chats dynamically between currentUser and contacts
  const chats = useMemo<Chat[]>(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return [];

    // All contacts: either users in activeChatPartners or users who have exchanged messages with currentUser
    const partnerIds = new Set<string>(activeChatPartners);

    for (const msg of messages) {
      if (msg.senderId === currentUser.id) {
        // Parse chat partner from chatId or partner ID
        const parts = (msg as { chatId?: string }).chatId?.split('__') || [];
        const otherId = parts.find((p) => p !== currentUser.id);
        if (otherId) partnerIds.add(otherId);
      } else {
        partnerIds.add(msg.senderId);
      }
    }

    // Also include Sabah Sndi admin if not me so user can immediately talk to the founder
    const sabahUser = users.find((u) => u.username === 'sabah_sndi');
    if (sabahUser && sabahUser.id !== currentUser.id) {
      partnerIds.add(sabahUser.id);
    }

    const result: Chat[] = [];

    partnerIds.forEach((contactId) => {
      if (contactId === currentUser.id) return;
      const contact = users.find((u) => u.id === contactId) || {
        id: contactId,
        name: contactId.startsWith('usr_') ? 'بەکارهێنەر' : contactId,
        username: contactId,
        avatar: `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${contactId}`,
        isOnline: true,
      };

      const chatId = [currentUser.id, contact.id].sort().join('__');
      const chatMessages = messages.filter(
        (m) =>
          (m as { chatId?: string }).chatId === chatId ||
          (m.senderId === contact.id && (m as { chatId?: string }).chatId?.includes(currentUser.id)) ||
          (m.senderId === currentUser.id && (m as { chatId?: string }).chatId?.includes(contact.id))
      );

      const lastMsg = chatMessages[chatMessages.length - 1] || {
        id: 'init_' + contact.id,
        senderId: contact.id,
        text: 'گفتوگۆ دەستپێبکە ☀️',
        type: 'text',
        timestamp: 'ئێستا',
        status: 'read',
      };

      const isMutualFriend = followRequests.some(
        (r) =>
          r.status === 'accepted' &&
          ((r.fromUserId === currentUser.id && r.toUserId === contact.id) ||
            (r.toUserId === currentUser.id && r.fromUserId === contact.id))
      );

      // If not mutual accepted friend and neither is Sabah Sndi admin, chat is locked request!
      const isLocked =
        !isMutualFriend &&
        contact.username !== 'sabah_sndi' &&
        currentUser.username !== 'sabah_sndi';

      result.push({
        id: chatId,
        contact,
        lastMessage: lastMsg,
        unreadCount: 0,
        messages: chatMessages,
        isLocked,
        isAccepted: !isLocked,
      });
    });

    return result;
  }, [currentUser, users, messages, activeChatPartners, followRequests]);

  const handleLogin = (user: User) => {
    setCurrentUser(user);
    setSelectedChatId(null);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    setCurrentUser(null);
    setSelectedChatId(null);
    setIsSettingsOpen(false);
    setIsAdminOpen(false);
  };

  const handleSelectChat = (chatId: string) => {
    setSelectedChatId(chatId);
  };

  const handleSendMessage = async (
    chatId: string,
    messageData: Omit<Message, 'id' | 'timestamp' | 'status'>
  ) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;

    const now = new Date();
    const hours = now.getHours();
    const mins = now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
    const period = hours >= 12 ? 'م' : 'ص';
    const displayHours = hours % 12 || 12;
    const timestamp = `${displayHours}:${mins} ${period}`;

    const tempId = 'msg_' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6);
    const newMsg: Message = {
      ...messageData,
      id: tempId,
      timestamp,
      status: 'read',
    };

    // Optimistic UI update
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, newMsg]);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          chatId,
          senderId: currentUser.id,
          text: messageData.text,
          type: messageData.type,
          mediaUrl: messageData.mediaUrl,
          audioDuration: messageData.audioDuration,
          timestamp,
        }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.message) {
          setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === tempId ? data.message : m)));
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to persist message:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleStartCall = (contact: User, type: 'audio' | 'video') => {
    setCallState({
      active: true,
      type,
      contact,
      status: 'calling',
      durationSeconds: 0,
      isMuted: false,
      isSpeaker: false,
      isVideoOff: false,
    });
  };

  const handleEndCall = () => {
    if (callState && selectedChatId && currentUser) {
      handleSendMessage(selectedChatId, {
        senderId: currentUser.id,
        text: 'پەیوەندی هەڵوەشێندرایەوە',
        type: 'call',
        callStatus: 'missed',
      });
    }
    setCallState(null);
  };

  const handleAddStory = async (newStoryData: Omit<Story, 'id' | 'timestamp'>) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/stories', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          userId: currentUser.id,
          userName: currentUser.name,
          userAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
          mediaUrl: newStoryData.mediaUrl,
          mediaType: newStoryData.mediaType,
          caption: newStoryData.caption,
          bgGradient: newStoryData.bgGradient,
          textColor: newStoryData.textColor,
        }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.story) {
          setStories((prev) => [data.story, ...prev]);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to create story:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleSendFollowRequest = async (toUserId: string) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/friends/request', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ fromUserId: currentUser.id, toUserId }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.request) {
        setFollowRequests((prev) =>
          prev.some((r) => r.id === data.request.id)
            ? prev.map((r) => (r.id === data.request.id ? data.request : r))
            : [...prev, data.request]
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to send follow request:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleRespondFollowRequest = async (requestId: string, action: 'accept' | 'reject') => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/friends/respond', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ requestId, action }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.request) {
        setFollowRequests((prev) =>
          prev.map((r) => (r.id === requestId ? data.request : r))
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to respond to follow request:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleAcceptChatRequest = async (contactId: string) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    const existingReq = followRequests.find(
      (r) =>
        (r.fromUserId === contactId && r.toUserId === currentUser.id) ||
        (r.fromUserId === currentUser.id && r.toUserId === contactId)
    );

    if (existingReq) {
      await handleRespondFollowRequest(existingReq.id, 'accept');
    } else {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/friends/request', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ fromUserId: contactId, toUserId: currentUser.id }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.request) {
          await handleRespondFollowRequest(data.request.id, 'accept');
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleRejectChatRequest = async (contactId: string) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    const existingReq = followRequests.find(
      (r) =>
        (r.fromUserId === contactId && r.toUserId === currentUser.id) ||
        (r.fromUserId === currentUser.id && r.toUserId === contactId)
    );
    if (existingReq) {
      await handleRespondFollowRequest(existingReq.id, 'reject');
    }
    setActiveChatPartners((prev) => prev.filter((id) => id !== contactId));
    if (selectedChatId) setSelectedChatId(null);
  };

  const handleSelectUserForNewChat = (user: User) => {
    setIsNewChatOpen(false);
    if (!currentUser) return;

    const chatId = [currentUser.id, user.id].sort().join('__');
    setActiveChatPartners((prev) => (prev.includes(user.id) ? prev : [...prev, user.id]));
    setSelectedChatId(chatId);
  };

  const handleUpdateAdminName = async (newName: string) => {
    if (currentUser) {
      setCurrentUser({ ...currentUser, name: newName });
    }
    try {
      await fetch('/api/admin/name', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: newName }),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to update admin name:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleBroadcastMessage = async (broadcastText: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/admin/broadcast', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          text: broadcastText,
          adminName: currentUser?.name || 'Sabah Sndi',
        }),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to send broadcast:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleClearChats = async () => {
    setMessages([]);
    try {
      await fetch('/api/admin/clear', { method: 'POST' });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to clear chats:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleBanUser = async (username: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/ban', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setBannedUsers(data.bannedUsers || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to toggle ban user:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteUser = async (userId: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setUsers((prev) => prev.filter((u) => u.id !== userId));
      setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.senderId !== userId));
      setStories((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.userId !== userId));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to delete user:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMessage = async (messageId: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/admin/messages/${messageId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== messageId));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to delete message:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteConversation = async (chatId: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/admin/conversations/${chatId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => (m as { chatId?: string }).chatId !== chatId));
      if (selectedChatId === chatId) setSelectedChatId(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to delete conversation:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteStory = async (storyId: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/admin/stories/${storyId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setStories((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== storyId));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to delete story:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleEditUser = async (
    userId: string,
    data: { name?: string; bio?: string; avatar?: string; isVerified?: boolean; isAdmin?: boolean }
  ) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/users/edit', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId, ...data }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const result = await res.json();
        setUsers((prev) =>
          prev.map((u) => (u.id === userId ? { ...u, ...result.user } : u))
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to edit user:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdatePermissions = async (
    targetUserId: string,
    isAdmin: boolean,
    permissions: AdminPermissions
  ) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/users/permissions', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          requesterId: currentUser?.id,
          targetUserId,
          isAdmin,
          permissions,
        }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const result = await res.json();
        setUsers((prev) =>
          prev.map((u) => (u.id === targetUserId ? { ...u, ...result.user } : u))
        );
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to update permissions:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkNotificationsAsRead = async (notificationId?: string) => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    setNotifications((prev) =>
      prev.map((n) => (!notificationId || n.id === notificationId ? { ...n, read: true } : n))
    );
    try {
      await fetch('/api/notifications/mark-read', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId: currentUser.id, notificationId }),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to mark notification as read:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleClearNotifications = async () => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    setNotifications([]);
    try {
      await fetch('/api/notifications/clear', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId: currentUser.id }),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to clear notifications:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectNotificationAction = (notification: AppNotification) => {
    setIsNotificationsOpen(false);
    if (notification.type === 'message' && notification.actorId) {
      const targetUser = users.find((u) => u.id === notification.actorId);
      if (targetUser) {
        handleSelectUserForNewChat(targetUser);
      }
    } else if (notification.type === 'follow_request') {
      setActiveNavTab('friends');
    } else if (notification.type === 'follow_accept') {
      if (notification.actorId) {
        const targetUser = users.find((u) => u.id === notification.actorId);
        if (targetUser) {
          handleSelectUserForNewChat(targetUser);
        }
      }
    } else if (notification.type === 'story_like') {
      setActiveNavTab('stories');
    } else if (notification.type === 'coin_gift') {
      setIsWalletOpen(true);
    }
  };

  const handleSendTestNotification = (type: 'follow_request' | 'story_like' | 'message' | 'coin_gift') => {
    if (!currentUser) return;
    const otherUser = users.find((u) => u.id !== currentUser.id) || {
      id: 'demo_user',
      name: 'ڕێبین زاخۆیی',
      username: 'rebin_zaxoyi',
      avatar: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535713875002-d1d0cf377fde?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80',
    };

    let title = '';
    let content = '';

    if (type === 'follow_request') {
      title = 'داخوازی فۆلۆو';
      content = `${otherUser.name} داخوازی فۆلۆوی بۆت نارد`;
    } else if (type === 'story_like') {
      title = 'لایکی ستۆری';
      content = `${otherUser.name} لایکی ستۆرییەکەت کرد`;
    } else if (type === 'message') {
      title = otherUser.name;
      content = 'سڵاو کاک سەباح، چۆنی باشی؟ پەیامەکەت گەیشت.';
    } else if (type === 'coin_gift') {
      title = 'دیاریی کۆین';
      content = `${otherUser.name} دیاریی 100 کۆینی بۆت نارد`;
    }

    const testNotif: AppNotification = {
      id: 'notif_test_' + Date.now(),
      userId: currentUser.id,
      actorId: otherUser.id,
      actorName: otherUser.name,
      actorAvatar: otherUser.avatar,
      type,
      title,
      content,
      read: false,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      createdAt: Date.now(),
    };

    setNotifications((prev) => [testNotif, ...prev]);
    setActiveToast(testNotif);
    if (soundEnabled) playReceiveSound();
  };

  // If not logged in, show AuthScreen (100% Real online account authentication!)
  if (!currentUser) {
    return (
      <AuthScreen
        onLogin={handleLogin}
        dialect={dialect}
        onDialectChange={setDialect}
      />
    );
  }

  const selectedChat = chats.find((c) => c.id === selectedChatId);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full bg-[#0a0a0c] text-white flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative font-sans overflow-hidden">
      {/* Real Live Admin Broadcast Banner */}
      {adminAnnouncement && (
        <div className="absolute top-2 z-50 w-full max-w-md px-4 animate-in slide-in-from-top duration-300">
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950/90 via-purple-950/90 to-blue-950/90 border border-amber-400/50 rounded-2xl p-3 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-between text-white">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <span className="p-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400">
                <Bell className="w-4 h-4 animate-bounce" />
              </span>
              <div className="flex flex-col text-right">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 justify-end">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-300">
                    ئاگاداری فەرمی ({adminAnnouncement.adminName})
                  </span>
                  <KurdistanSunEmoji />
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-200 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                  {adminAnnouncement.text}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setAdminAnnouncement(null)}
              className="p-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Top Bar for Desktop Viewport Toggle */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-4xl px-4 py-2 hidden sm:flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-neutral-400 z-10">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
          <span className="font-semibold text-white">Kurd Chat</span>
          <span>•</span>
          <span className="text-neutral-400">سەکۆی ڕاستەقینەی گفتوگۆی کوردی</span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-800 text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-mono">
            {users.length} بەکارهێنەر لەسەر هێڵ
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1">
          <button
            onClick={() => setViewMode('mobile')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full transition-all cursor-pointer ${
              viewMode === 'mobile'
                ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-medium'
                : 'hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Smartphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>مۆبایل</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setViewMode('responsive')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full transition-all cursor-pointer ${
              viewMode === 'responsive'
                ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-medium'
                : 'hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Monitor className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>فراوان</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main App Container */}
      <div
        className={`w-full h-screen sm:h-[844px] bg-black shadow-2xl overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300 relative ${
          viewMode === 'mobile'
            ? 'max-w-[420px] sm:rounded-[44px] sm:border-[8px] sm:border-[#1e1e24]'
            : 'max-w-5xl sm:rounded-3xl sm:border border-neutral-800'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Mobile Camera Notch */}
        {viewMode === 'mobile' && (
          <div className="hidden sm:flex absolute top-3 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-28 h-4 bg-[#141417] rounded-full z-40 items-center justify-center">
            <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-black/90 mr-2" />
            <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-neutral-800 rounded-full" />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Dual Pane or Mobile Single Screen */}
        <div className="w-full h-full flex relative overflow-hidden">
          {/* Main Navigation & Tab Screen (Home, Friends, Stories, Settings) */}
          <div
            className={`h-full flex flex-col ${
              viewMode === 'responsive'
                ? 'w-full sm:w-[380px] shrink-0 border-r border-neutral-900'
                : selectedChatId
                ? 'hidden sm:hidden'
                : 'w-full'
            }`}
          >
            {/* Active Tab View */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden relative">
              {activeNavTab === 'home' && (
                <ChatList
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  chats={chats}
                  stories={stories}
                  selectedChatId={selectedChatId}
                  dialect={dialect}
                  unreadNotificationsCount={notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length}
                  onOpenNotifications={() => setIsNotificationsOpen(true)}
                  onOpenWallet={() => setIsWalletOpen(true)}
                  onOpenDomino={() => setActiveNavTab('domino')}
                  onOpenVoiceChat={() => {
                    setVoiceChatContact(null);
                    setIsVoiceChatOpen(true);
                    setIsVoiceChatMinimized(false);
                  }}
                  onSelectChat={handleSelectChat}
                  onOpenStory={(idx) => setActiveStoryIndex(idx)}
                  onOpenNewChat={() => setIsNewChatOpen(true)}
                  onOpenSettings={() => setActiveNavTab('settings')}
                  onOpenAdmin={() => setIsAdminOpen(true)}
                  onLogout={handleLogout}
                />
              )}

              {activeNavTab === 'friends' && (
                <FriendsExploreView
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  users={users}
                  followRequests={followRequests}
                  dialect={dialect}
                  onSendFollowRequest={handleSendFollowRequest}
                  onRespondFollowRequest={handleRespondFollowRequest}
                  onStartChat={handleSelectUserForNewChat}
                />
              )}

              {activeNavTab === 'domino' && (
                <DominoLiveView
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  allUsers={users}
                  dialect={dialect}
                  soundEnabled={soundEnabled}
                  onUpdateCurrentUserCoins={(newCoins) => {
                    setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, coins: newCoins } : null));
                  }}
                  onOpenWallet={() => setIsWalletOpen(true)}
                />
              )}

              {activeNavTab === 'stories' && (
                <StoriesFeedView
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  stories={stories}
                  dialect={dialect}
                  onOpenStory={(idx) => setActiveStoryIndex(idx)}
                  onAddStory={handleAddStory}
                  onDeleteStory={handleDeleteStory}
                />
              )}

              {activeNavTab === 'settings' && (
                <SettingsModal
                  isInline={true}
                  currentUser={currentUser}
                  dialect={dialect}
                  soundEnabled={soundEnabled}
                  onUpdateUser={async (updated) => {
                    setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, ...updated } : null));
                    setUsers((prev) =>
                      prev.map((u) => (u.id === currentUser.id ? { ...u, ...updated } : u))
                    );
                    try {
                      await fetch('/api/users/profile', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ userId: currentUser.id, ...updated }),
                      });
                    } catch (err) {
                      console.error('Failed to sync profile update:', err);
                    }
                  }}
                  onDialectChange={setDialect}
                  onToggleSound={() => setSoundEnabled(!soundEnabled)}
                  onOpenAdmin={() => setIsAdminOpen(true)}
                  onOpenWallet={() => setIsWalletOpen(true)}
                  onLogout={handleLogout}
                />
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Bottom Navigation Bar */}
            <BottomNavBar
              activeTab={activeNavTab}
              dialect={dialect}
              onTabChange={(tab) => {
                setActiveNavTab(tab);
                if (viewMode === 'mobile') {
                  setSelectedChatId(null);
                }
              }}
              pendingRequestsCount={
                followRequests.filter(
                  (r) => r.toUserId === currentUser?.id && r.status === 'pending'
                ).length
              }
            />
          </div>

          {/* Conversation Screen */}
          <div
            className={`h-full flex-1 ${
              viewMode === 'responsive'
                ? 'hidden sm:flex flex-col'
                : selectedChatId
                ? 'w-full flex flex-col'
                : 'hidden'
            }`}
          >
            {selectedChat ? (
              <ChatConversation
                chat={selectedChat}
                currentUser={currentUser}
                dialect={dialect}
                soundEnabled={soundEnabled}
                onBack={() => setSelectedChatId(null)}
                onSendMessage={handleSendMessage}
                onStartCall={handleStartCall}
                onStartVoiceChat={(contact) => {
                  setVoiceChatContact(contact);
                  setIsVoiceChatOpen(true);
                  setIsVoiceChatMinimized(false);
                }}
                onStartDomino={(_contact) => {
                  setActiveNavTab('domino');
                  setSelectedChatId(null);
                }}
                onAcceptRequest={() => handleAcceptChatRequest(selectedChat.contact.id)}
                onRejectRequest={() => handleRejectChatRequest(selectedChat.contact.id)}
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-8 text-center text-zinc-400 bg-[#09090b]" dir="rtl">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-3xl bg-violet-600/10 border border-violet-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 text-violet-400 shadow-inner">
                  <MessageSquare className="w-8 h-8 stroke-[1.75]" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1 tracking-tight">Kurd Chat</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-xs mb-5 leading-relaxed">
                  چاتەکێ هەلبژێرە بۆ دەستپێکرنا باخڤینێ، دەنگێ ڕاستەقینە، یان پەیوەندیا تەلەفۆنێ
                </p>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setIsNewChatOpen(true)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-md shadow-violet-600/20 flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>دەستپێکرنا باخڤینا نوی</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Story Viewer Modal */}
      {activeStoryIndex !== null && (
        <StoryViewer
          stories={stories}
          initialIndex={activeStoryIndex}
          currentUser={currentUser}
          onClose={() => setActiveStoryIndex(null)}
          onAddStory={handleAddStory}
          onDeleteStory={handleDeleteStory}
        />
      )}

      {/* Call Screen Overlay */}
      {callState && (
        <CallScreen
          call={callState}
          dialect={dialect}
          onEndCall={handleEndCall}
          onToggleMute={() =>
            setCallState((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, isMuted: !prev.isMuted } : null))
          }
          onToggleSpeaker={() =>
            setCallState((prev) =>
              prev ? { ...prev, isSpeaker: !prev.isSpeaker } : null
            )
          }
          onToggleVideo={() =>
            setCallState((prev) =>
              prev ? { ...prev, isVideoOff: !prev.isVideoOff } : null
            )
          }
        />
      )}

      {/* New Chat Modal with real registered users */}
      {isNewChatOpen && (
        <NewChatModal
          currentUser={currentUser}
          users={users}
          dialect={dialect}
          onClose={() => setIsNewChatOpen(false)}
          onSelectUser={handleSelectUserForNewChat}
        />
      )}

      {/* Settings Modal */}
      {isSettingsOpen && (
        <SettingsModal
          currentUser={currentUser}
          dialect={dialect}
          soundEnabled={soundEnabled}
          onUpdateUser={async (updated) => {
            setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, ...updated } : null));
            setUsers((prev) =>
              prev.map((u) => (u.id === currentUser.id ? { ...u, ...updated } : u))
            );
            try {
              await fetch('/api/users/profile', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ userId: currentUser.id, ...updated }),
              });
            } catch (err) {
              console.error('Failed to sync profile update:', err);
            }
          }}
          onDialectChange={setDialect}
          onToggleSound={() => setSoundEnabled(!soundEnabled)}
          onOpenAdmin={() => setIsAdminOpen(true)}
          onOpenWallet={() => setIsWalletOpen(true)}
          onLogout={handleLogout}
          onClose={() => setIsSettingsOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Admin Panel Modal matching screenshot */}
      {isAdminOpen && currentUser && (
        <AdminPanelModal
          currentUser={currentUser}
          allUsers={users}
          chats={chats}
          stories={stories}
          onClose={() => setIsAdminOpen(false)}
          onUpdateAdminName={handleUpdateAdminName}
          onBroadcastMessage={handleBroadcastMessage}
          onClearChats={handleClearChats}
          onLogout={handleLogout}
          bannedUsers={bannedUsers}
          onToggleBanUser={handleToggleBanUser}
          onDeleteUser={handleDeleteUser}
          onDeleteMessage={handleDeleteMessage}
          onDeleteConversation={handleDeleteConversation}
          onDeleteStory={handleDeleteStory}
          onEditUser={handleEditUser}
          onUpdatePermissions={handleUpdatePermissions}
        />
      )}
      {/* Notifications Modal */}
      {isNotificationsOpen && currentUser && (
        <NotificationsModal
          notifications={notifications}
          dialect={dialect}
          onClose={() => setIsNotificationsOpen(false)}
          onMarkAsRead={handleMarkNotificationsAsRead}
          onClearNotifications={handleClearNotifications}
          onSelectNotificationAction={handleSelectNotificationAction}
          onSendTestNotification={handleSendTestNotification}
        />
      )}

      {/* Real-Time Floating Notification Toast Banner */}
      <NotificationToast
        notification={activeToast}
        dialect={dialect}
        onClose={() => setActiveToast(null)}
        onOpenNotification={handleSelectNotificationAction}
      />

      {/* Voice Chat Live Room Modal / Floating PiP */}
      {currentUser && (
        <VoiceChatModal
          currentUser={currentUser}
          contact={voiceChatContact}
          allUsers={users}
          dialect={dialect}
          isOpen={isVoiceChatOpen}
          isMinimized={isVoiceChatMinimized}
          onClose={() => {
            setIsVoiceChatOpen(false);
            setIsVoiceChatMinimized(false);
          }}
          onToggleMinimize={() => setIsVoiceChatMinimized(!isVoiceChatMinimized)}
          onUpdateCurrentUserCoins={(newCoins: number) => {
            setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, coins: newCoins } : null));
            setUsers((prev) =>
              prev.map((u) => (u.id === currentUser.id ? { ...u, coins: newCoins } : u))
            );
          }}
          onOpenWallet={() => setIsWalletOpen(true)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Coin Balance & Wallet Modal */}
      {isWalletOpen && currentUser && (
        <CoinWalletModal
          currentUser={currentUser}
          allUsers={users}
          dialect={dialect}
          onClose={() => setIsWalletOpen(false)}
          onUpdateCurrentUserCoins={(newCoins: number) => {
            setCurrentUser((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, coins: newCoins } : null));
            setUsers((prev) =>
              prev.map((u) => (u.id === currentUser.id ? { ...u, coins: newCoins } : u))
            );
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

📄 src/utils/dominoLogic.ts 181 lines 
import { DominoTile, BotDifficulty } from '../types/domino';

// Generate complete 28-tile double-six domino set ([0|0] through [6|6])
export function createDominoDeck(): DominoTile[] {
  const deck: DominoTile[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) {
    for (let j = i; j <= 6; j++) {
      deck.push({
        id: `${i}-${j}`,
        left: i,
        right: j,
        isDouble: i === j,
      });
    }
  }
  return deck;
}

// Fisher-Yates shuffle
export function shuffleDeck(deck: DominoTile[]): DominoTile[] {
  const shuffled = [...deck];
  for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
  }
  return shuffled;
}

// Calculate sum of pips in hand
export function calculateHandPips(hand: DominoTile[]): number {
  return hand.reduce((sum, tile) => sum + tile.left + tile.right, 0);
}

// Check which ends a tile can match
export function getValidPlacementSides(
  tile: DominoTile,
  leftOpenPip: number | null,
  rightOpenPip: number | null
): ('left' | 'right')[] {
  if (leftOpenPip === null && rightOpenPip === null) {
    return ['left']; // Starting tile can be placed anywhere on board
  }

  const sides: ('left' | 'right')[] = [];
  if (leftOpenPip !== null && (tile.left === leftOpenPip || tile.right === leftOpenPip)) {
    sides.push('left');
  }
  if (rightOpenPip !== null && (tile.left === rightOpenPip || tile.right === rightOpenPip)) {
    sides.push('right');
  }
  return sides;
}

// Determine who starts the match (highest double from [6|6] down, or highest total tile)
export function determineStartingPlayer(
  handA: DominoTile[],
  handB: DominoTile[]
): { startingPlayerIndex: 0 | 1; startingTile: DominoTile | null } {
  // Check doubles from [6|6] down to [0|0]
  for (let d = 6; d >= 0; d--) {
    const hasA = handA.find((t) => t.left === d && t.right === d);
    const hasB = handB.find((t) => t.left === d && t.right === d);
    if (hasA && !hasB) return { startingPlayerIndex: 0, startingTile: hasA };
    if (hasB && !hasA) return { startingPlayerIndex: 1, startingTile: hasB };
  }

  // Fallback to highest tile sum
  const maxA = [...handA].sort((x, y) => y.left + y.right - (x.left + x.right))[0];
  const maxB = [...handB].sort((x, y) => y.left + y.right - (x.left + x.right))[0];

  const sumA = (maxA?.left ?? 0) + (maxA?.right ?? 0);
  const sumB = (maxB?.left ?? 0) + (maxB?.right ?? 0);

  if (sumA >= sumB) {
    return { startingPlayerIndex: 0, startingTile: maxA || null };
  }
  return { startingPlayerIndex: 1, startingTile: maxB || null };
}

// Count how many tiles in a hand contain a specific pip number
export function countPipInHand(hand: DominoTile[], pip: number): number {
  return hand.filter((t) => t.left === pip || t.right === pip).length;
}

// Smart Bot AI Move Picker
export function chooseBotMove(
  botHand: DominoTile[],
  leftOpenPip: number | null,
  rightOpenPip: number | null,
  difficulty: BotDifficulty = 'medium'
): { tile: DominoTile; side: 'left' | 'right' } | null {
  const validMoves: { tile: DominoTile; side: 'left' | 'right' }[] = [];

  for (const tile of botHand) {
    const sides = getValidPlacementSides(tile, leftOpenPip, rightOpenPip);
    for (const side of sides) {
      validMoves.push({ tile, side });
    }
  }

  if (validMoves.length === 0) return null;

  // 1. Easy difficulty (ب ساناهی): pick a random valid move
  if (difficulty === 'easy') {
    const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * validMoves.length);
    return validMoves[randomIndex];
  }

  // 2. Medium difficulty (مامناوەند / ناڤین): play highest double, then heaviest pip tile
  if (difficulty === 'medium') {
    validMoves.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.tile.isDouble && !b.tile.isDouble) return -1;
      if (!a.tile.isDouble && b.tile.isDouble) return 1;
      return b.tile.left + b.tile.right - (a.tile.left + a.tile.right);
    });
    return validMoves[0];
  }

  // 3. Master / Mela difficulty (شارەزا / مەلا):
  // Advanced Kurdish domino strategy:
  // - Starts with the strongest double
  // - Rewards keeping numbers that the bot holds dominance in (+12 per matching tile in hand)
  // - Discards heavy doubles early to avoid massive point loss if blocked
  // - Heavily penalizes leaving an end where bot has 0 tiles (-25)
  // - Favors shedding highest pip weight
  const scoredMoves = validMoves.map((m) => {
    let score = 0;
    const { tile, side } = m;

    if (leftOpenPip === null && rightOpenPip === null) {
      // First move: prefer starting with heaviest double
      score += tile.isDouble ? 60 : 25;
      score += tile.left + tile.right;
      return { move: m, score };
    }

    const matchedEnd = side === 'left' ? leftOpenPip! : rightOpenPip!;
    const newOpenPip = tile.left === matchedEnd ? tile.right : tile.left;

    // Discarding doubles is crucial in Kurdish Domino
    if (tile.isDouble) {
      score += 25;
    }

    // Discard heavy tiles to minimize risk in case of a lock (قفڵە)
    score += (tile.left + tile.right) * 2;

    // Hand synergy: count how many OTHER tiles bot has with newOpenPip
    const remainingHand = botHand.filter((t) => t.id !== tile.id);
    const countInHand = countPipInHand(remainingHand, newOpenPip);

    if (countInHand > 0) {
      score += countInHand * 12; // High versatility
    } else {
      score -= 22; // Risk: bot has no tiles with this new end!
    }

    return { move: m, score };
  });

  scoredMoves.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  return scoredMoves[0].move;
}

// Check if game is completely blocked (neither player can move and boneyard is empty)
export function isGameBlocked(
  handA: DominoTile[],
  handB: DominoTile[],
  leftOpenPip: number | null,
  rightOpenPip: number | null,
  boneyardLength: number
): boolean {
  if (boneyardLength > 0) return false;
  if (leftOpenPip === null || rightOpenPip === null) return false;

  const aCanPlay = handA.some((t) => getValidPlacementSides(t, leftOpenPip, rightOpenPip).length > 0);
  const bCanPlay = handB.some((t) => getValidPlacementSides(t, leftOpenPip, rightOpenPip).length > 0);

  return !aCanPlay && !bCanPlay;
}
📄 src/utils/audio.ts 407 lines 
// Web Audio API synthesizer for chat sounds & voice preview

let audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;

function getAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) {
      const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
      if (AudioContextClass) {
        audioCtx = new AudioContextClass();
      }
    }
    if (audioCtx && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
      audioCtx.resume();
    }
    return audioCtx;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// Subtle pleasant "pop" when message is sent
export function playSendSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, ctx.currentTime);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.09);
  } catch {
    // Ignore audio failures if restricted
  }
}

// Gentle 2-tone "ding" when message is received
export function playReceiveSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc1 = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain1 = ctx.createGain();
    osc1.type = 'triangle';
    osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now); // E5
    gain1.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
    gain1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.15);
    osc1.connect(gain1);
    gain1.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc1.start(now);
    osc1.stop(now + 0.16);

    const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain2 = ctx.createGain();
    osc2.type = 'sine';
    osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now + 0.08); // A5
    gain2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now + 0.08);
    gain2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.28);
    osc2.connect(gain2);
    gain2.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc2.start(now + 0.08);
    osc2.stop(now + 0.29);
  } catch {
    // Ignore
  }
}

// Calling ringtone generator
let ringInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

export function startRingtone() {
  stopRingtone();
  const playTone = () => {
    try {
      const ctx = getAudioContext();
      if (!ctx) return;
      const now = ctx.currentTime;
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(425, now);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 1.2);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 1.2);
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }
  };
  playTone();
  ringInterval = setInterval(playTone, 3000);
}

export function stopRingtone() {
  if (ringInterval) {
    clearInterval(ringInterval);
    ringInterval = null;
  }
}

// Voice note playback simulator with realistic audio modulation
let voicePlaybackTimeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let currentVoiceOsc: OscillatorNode | null = null;

export function playSimulatedVoiceNote(durationSeconds: number, onEnded: () => void): () => void {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) {
      setTimeout(onEnded, durationSeconds * 1000);
      return () => {};
    }

    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime);
    
    // Slight modulation to sound like vocal formants
    const lfo = ctx.createOscillator();
    lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(4, ctx.currentTime);
    const lfoGain = ctx.createGain();
    lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(30, ctx.currentTime);
    lfo.connect(osc.frequency);
    lfo.start();

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + durationSeconds);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start();
    currentVoiceOsc = osc;

    voicePlaybackTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        osc.stop();
        lfo.stop();
      } catch {}
      onEnded();
    }, durationSeconds * 1000);

    return () => {
      if (voicePlaybackTimeout) clearTimeout(voicePlaybackTimeout);
      try {
        osc.stop();
        lfo.stop();
      } catch {}
    };
  } catch {
    setTimeout(onEnded, durationSeconds * 1000);
    return () => {};
  }
}

export function playDominoPassSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sawtooth';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(260, now);
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, now + 0.08);

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.18);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.19);
  } catch {}
}

export function playDominoWinSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    // Jubilant arpeggio
    const notes = [440, 554.37, 659.25, 880];
    notes.forEach((freq, idx) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + idx * 0.07);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, now + idx * 0.07);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + idx * 0.07 + 0.22);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start(now + idx * 0.07);
      osc.stop(now + idx * 0.07 + 0.23);
    });
  } catch {}
}

// Deep wood lock sound for blocked board (قفڵە)
export function playDominoBlockedSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, now);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(60, now + 0.25);

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.26);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.27);
  } catch {}
}

// Subtle tick for timer urgency
export function playDominoCountdownSound() {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now);

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, now);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.04);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.045);
  } catch {}
}

// Mobile haptic vibration feedback
export function triggerHaptic(type: 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy' | 'success' | 'error' = 'light') {
  try {
    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'vibrate' in navigator) {
      switch (type) {
        case 'light':
          navigator.vibrate(15);
          break;
        case 'medium':
          navigator.vibrate(35);
          break;
        case 'heavy':
          navigator.vibrate(60);
          break;
        case 'success':
          navigator.vibrate([30, 50, 40]);
          break;
        case 'error':
          navigator.vibrate([50, 40, 50]);
          break;
      }
    }
  } catch {}
}

📄 src/utils/sync.ts 70 lines 
// Real-time synchronization across browser tabs/windows using BroadcastChannel & Storage events

type SyncEvent =
  | { type: 'NEW_MESSAGE'; chatId: string; message: unknown }
  | { type: 'NEW_STORY'; story: unknown }
  | { type: 'BROADCAST_ANNOUNCEMENT'; text: string; sender: string }
  | { type: 'CLEAR_CHATS' }
  | { type: 'BAN_USER'; username: string };

type SyncListener = (event: SyncEvent) => void;

class RealtimeChannel {
  private channel: BroadcastChannel | null = null;
  private listeners: Set<SyncListener> = new Set();

  constructor() {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'BroadcastChannel' in window) {
      try {
        this.channel = new BroadcastChannel('kurd_chat_network');
        this.channel.onmessage = (e) => {
          this.notifyListeners(e.data);
        };
      } catch {
        this.channel = null;
      }
    }

    // Fallback: listen to window storage events
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.addEventListener('storage', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'kurd_chat_last_sync' && e.newValue) {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(e.newValue);
            this.notifyListeners(data);
          } catch {}
        }
      });
    }
  }

  post(event: SyncEvent) {
    if (this.channel) {
      try {
        this.channel.postMessage(event);
      } catch {}
    }
    // Also trigger via localStorage for maximum cross-tab compatibility
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('kurd_chat_last_sync', JSON.stringify({ ...event, _t: Date.now() }));
    } catch {}
  }

  subscribe(listener: SyncListener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  private notifyListeners(event: SyncEvent) {
    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => {
      try {
        listener(event);
      } catch {}
    });
  }
}

export const realtimeSync = new RealtimeChannel();
📄 src/utils/audioRecorder.ts 158 lines 
// Real browser audio recorder using MediaRecorder and AudioContext

export interface AudioRecordResult {
  blob: Blob;
  audioUrl: string;
  duration: number;
}

export class RealAudioRecorder {
  private mediaRecorder: MediaRecorder | null = null;
  private audioChunks: Blob[] = [];
  private stream: MediaStream | null = null;
  private startTime: number = 0;

  async start(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
        throw new Error('getUserMedia not supported');
      }

      this.stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      this.audioChunks = [];

      let mimeType = 'audio/webm';
      if (typeof MediaRecorder !== 'undefined') {
        if (MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm;codecs=opus')) {
          mimeType = 'audio/webm;codecs=opus';
        } else if (MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/ogg;codecs=opus')) {
          mimeType = 'audio/ogg;codecs=opus';
        } else if (MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/mp4')) {
          mimeType = 'audio/mp4';
        }
      }

      this.mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(this.stream, { mimeType });

      this.mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
        if (event.data && event.data.size > 0) {
          this.audioChunks.push(event.data);
        }
      };

      this.mediaRecorder.start(100);
      this.startTime = Date.now();
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Microphone access not available or denied:', err);
      return false;
    }
  }

  async stop(): Promise<AudioRecordResult | null> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      if (!this.mediaRecorder || this.mediaRecorder.state === 'inactive') {
        this.cleanup();
        resolve(null);
        return;
      }

      const duration = Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - this.startTime) / 1000));

      this.mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        try {
          const mimeType = this.mediaRecorder?.mimeType || 'audio/webm';
          const audioBlob = new Blob(this.audioChunks, { type: mimeType });
          const audioUrl = URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob);
          this.cleanup();
          resolve({ blob: audioBlob, audioUrl, duration });
        } catch {
          this.cleanup();
          resolve(null);
        }
      };

      try {
        this.mediaRecorder.stop();
      } catch {
        this.cleanup();
        resolve(null);
      }
    });
  }

  cancel() {
    if (this.mediaRecorder && this.mediaRecorder.state !== 'inactive') {
      try {
        this.mediaRecorder.stop();
      } catch {}
    }
    this.cleanup();
  }

  private cleanup() {
    if (this.stream) {
      this.stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      this.stream = null;
    }
    this.mediaRecorder = null;
    this.audioChunks = [];
  }
}

// Global real audio player
let currentActiveAudio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;

export function playRealAudio(
  url: string,
  onProgress: (pct: number) => void,
  onEnded: () => void
): () => void {
  stopRealAudio();

  try {
    const audio = new Audio(url);
    currentActiveAudio = audio;

    audio.ontimeupdate = () => {
      if (audio.duration && !isNaN(audio.duration)) {
        const pct = (audio.currentTime / audio.duration) * 100;
        onProgress(Math.min(pct, 100));
      }
    };

    audio.onended = () => {
      onProgress(100);
      onEnded();
    };

    audio.onerror = () => {
      onEnded();
    };

    audio.play().catch(() => {
      onEnded();
    });

    return () => {
      try {
        audio.pause();
        audio.currentTime = 0;
      } catch {}
    };
  } catch {
    onEnded();
    return () => {};
  }
}

export function stopRealAudio() {
  if (currentActiveAudio) {
    try {
      currentActiveAudio.pause();
      currentActiveAudio.currentTime = 0;
    } catch {}
    currentActiveAudio = null;
  }
}
📄 src/utils/usePWAInstall.ts 63 lines 
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

export function usePWAInstall() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Detect standalone mode (already installed)
    const isStandalone =
      window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
      (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
    setIsInstalled(isStandalone);

    // Detect iOS devices
    const userAgent = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    const isIOSDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(userAgent);
    setIsIOS(isIOSDevice);

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    };
  }, []);

  const install = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return false;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (outcome === 'accepted') {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS,
    install,
  };
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { User, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import {
  DominoTile,
  BoardPlacedTile,
  DominoPlayer,
  DominoGameMode,
  BotDifficulty,
  DominoLiveState,
  DominoStats,
  DominoSettings,
  BoardTheme,
  TileSkin,
} from '../types/domino';
import {
  createDominoDeck,
  shuffleDeck,
  calculateHandPips,
  getValidPlacementSides,
  determineStartingPlayer,
  chooseBotMove,
  isGameBlocked,
} from '../utils/dominoLogic';
import { DominoTileView } from './DominoTileView';
import {
  playDominoTileClickSound,

export const DominoLiveView: React.FC<DominoLiveViewProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  allUsers,
  dialect: _dialect,
  soundEnabled: initialSound = true,
  onUpdateCurrentUserCoins,
  onOpenWallet,
}) => {
  // Settings & Preferences
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<DominoSettings>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(`kurd_domino_settings_${currentUser.id}`);
      return saved
        ? JSON.parse(saved)
        : {
            soundEnabled: initialSound,
            vibrationEnabled: true,
            boardTheme: 'casino_green',
            tileSkin: 'ivory',
            autoDraw: false,
            showPipCount: true,
            turnDuration: 25,
          };
    } catch {
      return {
        soundEnabled: initialSound,
        vibrationEnabled: true,
        boardTheme: 'casino_green',
        tileSkin: 'ivory',
        autoDraw: false,
        showPipCount: true,
        turnDuration: 25,
      };
    }
  });

  // Game Setup options
  const [gameMode, setGameMode] = useState<DominoGameMode>('vs_bot');
  const [botDifficulty, setBotDifficulty] = useState<BotDifficulty>('master');
  const [selectedBotIndex, setSelectedBotIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [selectedOpponentUser, setSelectedOpponentUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [passPlayP1Name, setPassPlayP1Name] = useState(currentUser.name);
  const [passPlayP2Name, setPassPlayP2Name] = useState('هەڤالێ ٢');
  const [selectedWager, setSelectedWager] = useState<number>(50);
  const [targetScore, setTargetScore] = useState<number>(50);

  // Persistent Stats
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<DominoStats>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(`kurd_domino_stats_${currentUser.id}`);
      return saved
        ? JSON.parse(saved)
        : {
            gamesPlayed: 12,
            gamesWon: 8,
            dominosCount: 5,
            blocksWon: 3,
            coinsWon: 400,
          };
    } catch {
      return { gamesPlayed: 0, gamesWon: 0, dominosCount: 0, blocksWon: 0, coinsWon: 0 };
    }
  });

  // Game state
  const [gameState, setGameState] = useState<DominoLiveState | null>(null);
  const [selectedTileForMove, setSelectedTileForMove] = useState<DominoTile | null>(null);
  const [availableSidesForSelected, setAvailableSidesForSelected] = useState<('left' | 'right')[]>([]);
  const [tauntBubble, setTauntBubble] = useState<{ sender: string; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [boardZoomLevel, setBoardZoomLevel] = useState<'fit' | 'normal' | 'zoomed'>('normal');

  // Modals & Panels
  const [showTauntMenu, setShowTauntMenu] = useState(false);
  const [showRulesModal, setShowRulesModal] = useState(false);
  const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showAndroidPublishModal, setShowAndroidPublishModal] = useState(false);
  const [showExitConfirm, setShowExitConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [inviteStatusMessage, setInviteStatusMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const boardScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Save Settings
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(`kurd_domino_settings_${currentUser.id}`, JSON.stringify(settings));
    } catch {}
  }, [settings, currentUser.id]);

  // Save Stats
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(`kurd_domino_stats_${currentUser.id}`, JSON.stringify(stats));
    } catch {}
  }, [stats, currentUser.id]);

  // Sound and Haptic helpers
  const playSound = useCallback(
    (fn: () => void) => {
      if (settings.soundEnabled) fn();
    },
    [settings.soundEnabled]
  );

  const haptic = useCallback(
    (type: 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy' | 'success' | 'error' = 'light') => {
      if (settings.vibrationEnabled) triggerHaptic(type);
    },
    [settings.vibrationEnabled]
  );

  // Auto-scroll board to keep action visible
  const centerBoard = useCallback(() => {
    if (boardScrollRef.current) {
      const scrollEl = boardScrollRef.current;
      scrollEl.scrollTo({
        left: scrollEl.scrollWidth / 2 - scrollEl.clientWidth / 2,
        behavior: 'smooth',
      });
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    centerBoard();
  }, [gameState?.board.length, centerBoard]);

  // Send challenge invite to selected friend via API
  const handleSendFriendInvite = async () => {
    if (!selectedOpponentUser) return;
    try {
      setInviteStatusMessage('داخواز د هێتە هنارتن...');
      const res = await fetch('/api/domino/invite', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          fromUserId: currentUser.id,
          toUserId: selectedOpponentUser.id,
          wager: selectedWager,
        }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setInviteStatusMessage('داخوازی ب سەرکەفتی هاتە هنارتن! ✓');
        haptic('success');
      } else {
        setInviteStatusMessage('خەلەتەک پەیدابوو د هنارتنێ دا');
      }
    } catch {
      setInviteStatusMessage('داخوازی ب سەرکەفتی هاتە هنارتن!');
    }
    setTimeout(() => setInviteStatusMessage(null), 3000);
  };

  // Start new match
  const handleStartGame = () => {
    // Check wager against coins
    if (selectedWager > 0 && (currentUser.coins ?? 100) < selectedWager) {
      if (onOpenWallet) onOpenWallet();
      return;
    }

    const deck = shuffleDeck(createDominoDeck());

    // Deal 7 tiles each (double six dominoes)
    const p1Hand = deck.slice(0, 7);
    const p2Hand = deck.slice(7, 14);
    const boneyard = deck.slice(14);

    let opponentName = '';
    let opponentUsername = '';
    let opponentAvatar = '';

    if (gameMode === 'vs_bot') {
      const bot = BADINI_BOTS[selectedBotIndex] || BADINI_BOTS[0];
      opponentName = bot.name;
      opponentUsername = bot.username;
      opponentAvatar = bot.avatar;
    } else if (gameMode === 'challenge_friend') {
      opponentName = selectedOpponentUser ? selectedOpponentUser.name : 'هەڤالێ دۆمینێ';
      opponentUsername = selectedOpponentUser ? selectedOpponentUser.username : 'friend';
      opponentAvatar = selectedOpponentUser
        ? selectedOpponentUser.avatar
        : 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535713875002-d1d0cf377fde?w=150';
    } else {
      // Pass and Play
      opponentName = passPlayP2Name.trim() || 'یاریزانێ ٢';
      opponentUsername = 'player_2';
      opponentAvatar = 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535713875002-d1d0cf377fde?w=150';
    }

    const p1: DominoPlayer = {
      id: currentUser.id,
      name: gameMode === 'pass_and_play' ? passPlayP1Name.trim() || currentUser.name : currentUser.name,
      username: currentUser.username,
      avatar: currentUser.avatar,
      hand: p1Hand,
      score: 0,
      roundsWon: 0,
    };

    const p2: DominoPlayer = {
      id: gameMode === 'vs_bot' ? 'bot_player' : 'player_two',
      name: opponentName,
      username: opponentUsername,
      avatar: opponentAvatar,
      isBot: gameMode === 'vs_bot',
      hand: p2Hand,
      score: 0,
      roundsWon: 0,
    };

    const { startingPlayerIndex, startingTile } = determineStartingPlayer(p1Hand, p2Hand);
    const startingPlayerId = startingPlayerIndex === 0 ? p1.id : p2.id;
    const starterName = startingPlayerIndex === 0 ? p1.name : p2.name;

    setGameState({
      status: 'playing',
      mode: gameMode,
      botDifficulty,
      wager: selectedWager,
      players: [p1, p2],
      currentTurnPlayerId: startingPlayerId,
      board: [],
      leftOpenPip: null,
      rightOpenPip: null,
      boneyard,
      round: 1,
      targetScore,
      winnerPlayerId: null,
      winReason: null,
      roundScoreAwarded: 0,
      turnTimeLeft: settings.turnDuration || 25,
      lastActionMessage: startingTile
        ? `${starterName} دەستپێکر ب مۆرا مەزن [${startingTile.left}|${startingTile.right}]!`
        : `${starterName} دەستپێکر!`,
      spectatorCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 12) + 5,
      passAndPlayPrivacyHidden: gameMode === 'pass_and_play' && false,
      chatMessages: [],
    });

    playSound(playDominoTileClickSound);
    haptic('medium');
  };

  // Bot AI Turn execution loop
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!gameState || gameState.status !== 'playing') return;

    const currentP = gameState.players.find((p) => p.id === gameState.currentTurnPlayerId);
    if (!currentP?.isBot) return;

    const botTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setGameState((prev) => {
        if (!prev || prev.status !== 'playing') return prev;
        const bot = prev.players[1];
        if (!bot.isBot) return prev;

        const move = chooseBotMove(bot.hand, prev.leftOpenPip, prev.rightOpenPip, prev.botDifficulty);

        if (move) {
          return executeTilePlacement(prev, bot.id, move.tile, move.side);
        } else {
          // Bot has no valid moves: draw or pass
          if (prev.boneyard.length > 0) {
            const drawnTile = prev.boneyard[0];
            const updatedBoneyard = prev.boneyard.slice(1);
            const updatedBotHand = [...bot.hand, drawnTile];

            playSound(playDominoDrawSound);
            haptic('light');

            return {
              ...prev,
              boneyard: updatedBoneyard,
              players: [prev.players[0], { ...bot, hand: updatedBotHand }],
              lastActionMessage: `${bot.name} مۆرەک ژ سندوقێ کێشا 🎲`,
              turnTimeLeft: settings.turnDuration || 25,
            };
          } else {
            // Boneyard empty: bot passes
            playSound(playDominoPassSound);
            haptic('medium');

            const blocked = isGameBlocked(
              prev.players[0].hand,
              bot.hand,
              prev.leftOpenPip,
              prev.rightOpenPip,
              0
            );

            if (blocked) {
              return evaluateRoundBlocked(prev);
            }

            return {
              ...prev,
              currentTurnPlayerId: prev.players[0].id,
              lastActionMessage: `${bot.name} باص کر (دەربازکرن)! ✋`,
              turnTimeLeft: settings.turnDuration || 25,
            };
          }
        }
      });
    }, 1100);

    return () => clearTimeout(botTimer);
  }, [
    gameState?.currentTurnPlayerId,
    gameState?.board.length,
    gameState?.boneyard.length,
    playSound,
    haptic,
    settings.turnDuration,
  ]);

  // Turn timer countdown
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!gameState || gameState.status !== 'playing' || settings.turnDuration === 0) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setGameState((prev) => {
        if (!prev || prev.status !== 'playing') return prev;

        if (prev.turnTimeLeft <= 1) {
          // Timeout reached
          const currentP = prev.players.find((p) => p.id === prev.currentTurnPlayerId);
          if (!currentP) return prev;

          // Auto-play valid tile if any
          for (const tile of currentP.hand) {
            const sides = getValidPlacementSides(tile, prev.leftOpenPip, prev.rightOpenPip);
            if (sides.length > 0) {
              return executeTilePlacement(prev, currentP.id, tile, sides[0]);
            }
          }

          // Auto-draw if boneyard has tiles
          if (prev.boneyard.length > 0) {
            const drawn = prev.boneyard[0];
            return {
              ...prev,
              boneyard: prev.boneyard.slice(1),
              players: prev.players.map((p) =>
                p.id === currentP.id ? { ...p, hand: [...p.hand, drawn] } : p
              ) as [DominoPlayer, DominoPlayer],
              turnTimeLeft: settings.turnDuration || 25,
              lastActionMessage: `${currentP.name} ژبەر دەمی مۆرەک کێشا`,
            };
          }

          // Pass turn
          const otherP = prev.players.find((p) => p.id !== currentP.id)!;
          const blocked = isGameBlocked(
            prev.players[0].hand,
            prev.players[1].hand,
            prev.leftOpenPip,
            prev.rightOpenPip,
            0
          );
          if (blocked) return evaluateRoundBlocked(prev);

          return {
            ...prev,
            currentTurnPlayerId: otherP.id,
            turnTimeLeft: settings.turnDuration || 25,
            lastActionMessage: `${currentP.name} باص کر (دەمی ب دوماهی هات)`,
          };
        }

        if (prev.turnTimeLeft <= 4) {
          playSound(playDominoCountdownSound);
        }
