📄 src/components/CallScreen.tsx 206 lines 
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CallState, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import { translations } from '../utils/i18n';
import {
  PhoneOff,
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  Video,
  VideoOff,
  PhoneCall,
  Shield,
  Radio,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { startRingtone, stopRingtone } from '../utils/audio';

interface CallScreenProps {
  call: CallState;
  dialect: Dialect;
  onEndCall: () => void;
  onToggleMute: () => void;
  onToggleSpeaker: () => void;
  onToggleVideo: () => void;
}

export const CallScreen: React.FC<CallScreenProps> = ({
  call,
  dialect,
  onEndCall,
  onToggleMute,
  onToggleSpeaker,
  onToggleVideo,
}) => {
  const t = translations[dialect];
  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [callStatus, setCallStatus] = useState<'calling' | 'ringing' | 'connected'>('calling');

  useEffect(() => {
    startRingtone();
    const ringingTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setCallStatus('ringing');
    }, 1500);

    const connectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      stopRingtone();
      setCallStatus('connected');
    }, 3800);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(ringingTimer);
      clearTimeout(connectTimer);
      stopRingtone();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let interval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
    if (callStatus === 'connected') {
      interval = setInterval(() => {
        setSeconds((prev) => prev + 1);
      }, 1000);
    }
    return () => {
      if (interval) clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [callStatus]);

  const formatDuration = (totalSeconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
    const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[#09090d]/95 backdrop-blur-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-6 sm:p-10 select-none text-zinc-100" dir="rtl">
      {/* Background ambient lighting */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-violet-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-80 h-80 bg-indigo-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Top Header */}
      <div className="z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center mt-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs text-zinc-300 shadow-sm">
          <PhoneCall className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-violet-400 animate-pulse" />
          <span className="font-semibold tracking-tight">
            {call.type === 'video' ? t.videoCall : t.audioCall}
          </span>
        </div>

        <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-white mt-2 tracking-tight">
          {call.contact.name}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          {callStatus === 'calling' && t.calling}
          {callStatus === 'ringing' && t.ringing}
          {callStatus === 'connected' && (
            <span className="text-emerald-400 font-mono tracking-wider font-bold">
              {formatDuration(seconds)}
            </span>
          )}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Center Avatar with animated soundwave rings */}
      <div className="z-10 flex flex-col items-center my-auto">
        <div className="relative">
          {callStatus === 'calling' || callStatus === 'ringing' ? (
            <>
              <div className="absolute -inset-3 rounded-full bg-violet-600/20 animate-ping duration-1000" />
              <div className="absolute -inset-6 rounded-full bg-indigo-600/15 animate-pulse" />
            </>
          ) : (
            <div className="absolute -inset-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 animate-pulse" />
          )}

          {call.type === 'video' && !call.isVideoOff ? (
            <div className="w-48 h-64 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-violet-500/70 shadow-2xl relative bg-zinc-950">
              <img
                src={call.contact.avatar || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1534528741775-53994a69daeb?auto=format&fit=crop&w=400&q=80'}
                alt={call.contact.name}
                className="w-full h-full object-cover"
              />
              <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-md px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] text-zinc-300 font-mono">
                HD 1080p
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="relative">
              <img
                src={call.contact.avatar || `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${call.contact.id}`}
                alt={call.contact.name}
                className="w-36 h-36 rounded-full object-cover ring-4 ring-zinc-700/80 shadow-2xl"
              />
              {callStatus === 'connected' && (
                <span className="absolute bottom-1 right-1 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 border-2 border-[#09090d] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Radio className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-zinc-950" />
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {callStatus === 'connected' && (
          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-400 bg-zinc-900/90 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-zinc-800 shadow-xs">
            <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>پەیوەندی پارێزراوە (End-to-End Encrypted)</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Controls */}
      <div className="z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-5 mb-8 w-full max-w-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 w-full">
          {/* Mute Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleMute}
            className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 ${
              call.isMuted
                ? 'bg-rose-950/80 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800'
                : 'bg-zinc-900/90 text-zinc-200 hover:text-white border border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-800'
            }`}
          >
            {call.isMuted ? <MicOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Mic className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>

          {/* Speaker Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleSpeaker}
            className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 ${
              call.isSpeaker
                ? 'bg-violet-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-violet-600/30'
                : 'bg-zinc-900/90 text-zinc-200 hover:text-white border border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-800'
            }`}
          >
            {call.isSpeaker ? <Volume2 className="w-5 h-5" /> : <VolumeX className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>

          {/* Video Toggle Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleVideo}
            className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 ${
              call.isVideoOff
                ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-500 border border-zinc-800'
                : 'bg-zinc-900/90 text-zinc-200 hover:text-white border border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-800'
            }`}
          >
            {call.isVideoOff ? <VideoOff className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Video className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* End Call Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onEndCall}
          className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 active:scale-95 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-xl shadow-rose-600/40 transition-all cursor-pointer"
        >
          <PhoneOff className="w-7 h-7" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
📄 src/components/FriendsExploreView.tsx 329 lines 
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { User, FollowRequest, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import { translations } from '../utils/i18n';
import { UserBadges, KurdistanShield } from './KurdistanBadge';
import { Search, UserPlus, Check, X, Clock, MessageSquare, Shield, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

interface FriendsExploreViewProps {
  currentUser: User;
  users: User[];
  followRequests: FollowRequest[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  onSendFollowRequest: (toUserId: string) => void;
  onRespondFollowRequest: (requestId: string, action: 'accept' | 'reject') => void;
  onStartChat: (user: User) => void;
}

export const FriendsExploreView: React.FC<FriendsExploreViewProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  users,
  followRequests,
  dialect,
  onSendFollowRequest,
  onRespondFollowRequest,
  onStartChat,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'requests'>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const t = translations[dialect];

  // Incoming pending requests directed to the current user
  const incomingRequests = followRequests.filter(
    (r) => r.toUserId === currentUser.id && r.status === 'pending'
  );

  // Filter users by search
  const filteredUsers = users.filter((u) => {
    if (u.id === currentUser.id) return false;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return (
      u.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      u.username.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      (u.bio && u.bio.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    );
  });

  // Helper to determine relationship state with a user
  const getRelationState = (otherUserId: string) => {
    // Check incoming request
    const incoming = followRequests.find(
      (r) => r.fromUserId === otherUserId && r.toUserId === currentUser.id
    );
    if (incoming) {
      return { type: 'incoming', request: incoming };
    }

    // Check outgoing request
    const outgoing = followRequests.find(
      (r) => r.fromUserId === currentUser.id && r.toUserId === otherUserId
    );
    if (outgoing) {
      return { type: 'outgoing', request: outgoing };
    }

    return { type: 'none', request: null };
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full w-full bg-[#09090b] text-zinc-100 select-none overflow-hidden" dir="rtl">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="px-4 pt-4 pb-2 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-900/80 bg-[#09090b]/80 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>{t.friends || 'هەڤال'}</span>
            <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-violet-950/80 border border-violet-800 text-violet-300 font-mono">
              {users.length}
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 mt-0.5">
            هەمی کەسێن ل سەرخەت و پەسەندکرنا داخوازێن فۆلۆو
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Incoming badge trigger */}
        {incomingRequests.length > 0 && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('requests')}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-pink-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-pink-500/20 animate-pulse cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{incomingRequests.length} داخواز</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-neutral-900/60 bg-neutral-950/40">
        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('all')}
          className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer ${
            activeTab === 'all'
              ? 'bg-[#1e1e24] text-white border border-neutral-700/80'
              : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
          }`}
        >
          {t.allUsers || 'هەمی بکارهێنەر'} ({users.length - 1 > 0 ? users.length - 1 : 0})
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('requests')}
          className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer relative ${
            activeTab === 'requests'
              ? 'bg-[#1e1e24] text-white border border-neutral-700/80'
              : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
          }`}
        >
          <span>{t.incomingRequests || 'داخوازێن هاتین'}</span>
          {incomingRequests.length > 0 && (
            <span className="mr-1.5 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-pink-600 text-[10px] text-white font-mono">
              {incomingRequests.length}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Search Input */}
      {activeTab === 'all' && (
        <div className="px-4 py-2.5">
          <div className="relative flex items-center bg-[#1c1c1e] rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2 border border-neutral-800/80 focus-within:border-purple-500 transition-all">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 shrink-0 ml-2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="گەڕیان ب ناڤ یان Username..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-white placeholder-neutral-400 text-xs focus:outline-none text-right font-sans"
              dir="rtl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Content List */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-2 space-y-2.5 no-scrollbar">
        {/* Tab 1: All Registered Users */}
        {activeTab === 'all' && (
          <>
            {filteredUsers.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-12 text-neutral-500 text-xs">
                چ کەس نەهاتنە دیتن
              </div>
            ) : (
              filteredUsers.map((user) => {
                const relation = getRelationState(user.id);
                const isFriend = relation.request?.status === 'accepted';
                const isPendingOutgoing = relation.type === 'outgoing' && relation.request?.status === 'pending';
                const isPendingIncoming = relation.type === 'incoming' && relation.request?.status === 'pending';

                return (
                  <div
                    key={user.id}
                    className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#141418] border border-neutral-800/80 hover:border-neutral-700 transition-all flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
                  >
                    {/* User Profile Info */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                      <div className="relative shrink-0">
                        {user.avatar ? (
                          <img
                            src={user.avatar}
                            alt={user.name}
                            className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ring-neutral-800"
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-purple-900 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-white">
                            {user.name[0]}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        {user.isOnline && (
                          <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 bg-emerald-500 border-2 border-black rounded-full" />
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex flex-col min-w-0 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">
                            {user.name}
                          </span>
                          <UserBadges user={user} />
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 font-mono">
                          @{user.username}
                        </span>
                        {user.bio && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 truncate mt-0.5 max-w-[170px]">
                            {user.bio}
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Action Controls */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                      {isFriend ? (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-800/60 text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
                            <span>{t.following || 'هەڤال'}</span>
                          </span>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => onStartChat(user)}
                            title="چات"
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      ) : isPendingOutgoing ? (
                        <button
                          disabled
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 opacity-80 cursor-default"
                        >
                          <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                          <span>داخواز هنارت</span>
                        </button>
                      ) : isPendingIncoming && relation.request ? (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => onRespondFollowRequest(relation.request!.id, 'accept')}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-[11px] font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            {t.accept || 'پەسەندکرن'}
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => onRespondFollowRequest(relation.request!.id, 'reject')}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-rose-950 text-zinc-400 hover:text-rose-400 border border-zinc-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => onSendFollowRequest(user.id)}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm shadow-violet-600/20 transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
                        >
                          <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                          <span>{t.follow || 'فۆلۆو'}</span>
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </>
        )}

        {/* Tab 2: Incoming Follow Requests */}
        {activeTab === 'requests' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {incomingRequests.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-12 text-neutral-500 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                <Clock className="w-8 h-8 text-neutral-600" />
                <span className="text-xs">{t.noRequests || 'چ داخوازێن نوو نینن'}</span>
              </div>
            ) : (

                        <span>{t.reject || 'ڕەتکرن'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  Type,
  Plus,

} from 'lucide-react';

interface StoriesFeedViewProps {
  currentUser: User;
  stories: Story[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  onOpenStory: (index: number) => void;
  onAddStory: (story: Omit<Story, 'id' | 'timestamp'>) => void;
  onDeleteStory?: (storyId: string) => void;

  {
    id: 'kurd_flag',
    name: 'ئاڵایێ کوردستانێ ☀️',

export const StoriesFeedView: React.FC<StoriesFeedViewProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  stories,
  dialect,
  onOpenStory,
  onAddStory,
  onDeleteStory,
}) => {
  const [isCreatorOpen, setIsCreatorOpen] = useState(false);
  const [creatorMode, setCreatorMode] = useState<'mobile_photo' | 'text_template'>('mobile_photo');
  const [selectedTemplate, setSelectedTemplate] = useState(TEMPLATE_PRESETS[0]);
  const [storyText, setStoryText] = useState('');
  const [uploadedImage, setUploadedImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState('');
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const t = translations[dialect];

  const handleImageFileSelect = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const res = ev.target?.result as string;
      if (res) setUploadedImage(res);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handlePublish = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (creatorMode === 'mobile_photo') {
      if (!uploadedImage) return;
      onAddStory({
        userId: currentUser.id,
        userName: currentUser.name,
        userAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
        mediaUrl: uploadedImage,
        mediaType: 'image',
        caption: caption.trim() || undefined,
        viewed: false,
      });
    } else {
      if (!storyText.trim()) return;
      onAddStory({
        userId: currentUser.id,
        userName: currentUser.name,
        userAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
        mediaType: 'text',
        caption: storyText.trim(),
        bgGradient: selectedTemplate.gradient,
        textColor: selectedTemplate.textColor,
        viewed: false,
      });
    }

    // Reset and close
    setIsCreatorOpen(false);
    setUploadedImage(null);
    setStoryText('');
    setCaption('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full w-full bg-[#09090b] text-zinc-100 select-none overflow-hidden" dir="rtl">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <div className="px-4 pt-4 pb-2.5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-900/80 bg-[#09090b]/80 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>{t.storiesTab || 'ستۆری'}</span>
            <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-violet-950/80 border border-violet-800 text-violet-300 font-mono">
              {stories.length}
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            ستۆریێت هەڤالان و بەلاڤکرنا ستۆریا نوی
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* New Story Quick Button */}
        <button
          onClick={() => setIsCreatorOpen(true)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-violet-600/30 cursor-pointer active:scale-95 transition-all"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>ستۆریێ نوی</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stories Grid */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4 no-scrollbar">
        {/* Creator Banner Card */}
        <div
          onClick={() => setIsCreatorOpen(true)}
          className="p-4 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#1e152d] via-[#161426] to-[#121217] border border-purple-800/40 hover:border-purple-600/70 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between shadow-xl"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="relative p-0.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr from-purple-500 to-pink-500">
              <img
                src={currentUser.avatar || `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${currentUser.username}`}
                alt={currentUser.name}
                className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover"
              />
              <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-blue-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-bold border-2 border-black">

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Story, User } from '../types/chat';
import { X, Heart, Send, Plus, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Sparkles, Trash2, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { KurdChatLogo } from './KurdChatLogo';
import { BlueVerifiedBadge } from './KurdistanBadge';

interface StoryViewerProps {
  stories: Story[];
  initialIndex: number;
  currentUser: User;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAddStory: (newStory: Omit<Story, 'id' | 'timestamp'>) => void;
  onDeleteStory?: (storyId: string) => void;
}

export const StoryViewer: React.FC<StoryViewerProps> = ({
  stories,
  initialIndex,
  currentUser,
  onClose,
  onAddStory,
  onDeleteStory,
}) => {
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(
    stories.length > 0
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(initialIndex === -1 ? 0 : initialIndex, stories.length - 1))
      : 0
  );
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState(false);
  const [showAddStory, setShowAddStory] = useState(initialIndex === -1 || stories.length === 0);
  const [newCaption, setNewCaption] = useState('');
  const [selectedBg, setSelectedBg] = useState('gradient-1');
  const currentStory = stories[currentIndex];
  const [liked, setLiked] = useState(() => currentStory?.likedBy?.includes(currentUser.id) || false);
  const [customStoryImage, setCustomStoryImage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setProgress(0);
    setLiked(Boolean(currentStory?.likedBy?.includes(currentUser.id)));
  }, [currentIndex, currentStory, currentUser.id]);

  const handleToggleLike = async () => {
    if (!currentStory) return;
    const nextLiked = !liked;
    setLiked(nextLiked);

    try {
      await fetch('/api/stories/like', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          storyId: currentStory.id,
          userId: currentUser.id,
          userName: currentUser.name,
          userAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
        }),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error toggling story like:', err);
    }
  };

  // Handle auto-advancing progress safely
  useEffect(() => {
    if (showAddStory || isPaused || !stories.length) return;

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress((prev) => {
        if (prev >= 100) return 100;
        return prev + 2;
      });
    }, 100);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [currentIndex, isPaused, showAddStory, stories.length]);

  // Handle story transition or closing outside of state reducers
  useEffect(() => {
    if (progress >= 100) {
      if (currentIndex < stories.length - 1) {
        setCurrentIndex((curr) => curr + 1);
        setProgress(0);
      } else {
        onClose();
      }
    }
  }, [progress, currentIndex, stories.length, onClose]);

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentIndex < stories.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex(currentIndex + 1);
      setProgress(0);
    } else {
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      setCurrentIndex(currentIndex - 1);
      setProgress(0);
    }
  };

  const handleStoryImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const result = ev.target?.result as string;
      if (result) {
        setCustomStoryImage(result);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleCreateStory = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newCaption.trim() && !customStoryImage) return;

    const bgMap: { [key: string]: string } = {
      'gradient-1': 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1579783900882-c0d3dad7b119?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
      'gradient-2': 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1506744038136-46273834b3fb?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
      'gradient-3': 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1464822759023-fed622ff2c3b?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80',
    };

    onAddStory({
      userId: currentUser.id,
      userName: currentUser.name,
      userAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
      mediaUrl: customStoryImage || bgMap[selectedBg] || bgMap['gradient-1'],
      mediaType: 'image',
      caption: newCaption.trim(),
      viewed: false,
    });

    setShowAddStory(false);
    setNewCaption('');
    setCustomStoryImage(null);
    onClose();
  };

  if (showAddStory || !currentStory) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4 select-none" dir="rtl">
        <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#121217] border border-zinc-800 rounded-3xl p-5 flex flex-col gap-4 text-white shadow-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-zinc-800/80 pb-3">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-violet-400" />
              <span>دانانا ستۆریێ نوی (بڵاوکردنەوەی ستۆری)</span>
            </h3>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                if (stories.length > 0 && initialIndex !== -1) {
                  setShowAddStory(false);
                } else {
                  onClose();
                }
              }}
              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer border border-zinc-800"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleCreateStory} className="flex flex-col gap-3.5">
            <textarea
              placeholder="دەقی ستۆری بنووسە... (ڕۆژێکی خۆش، پیرۆزبایی، یان وێنە)"
              value={newCaption}
              onChange={(e) => setNewCaption(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-24 bg-zinc-900/80 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs focus:border-violet-500 focus:outline-none resize-none text-white placeholder-zinc-500 leading-relaxed font-sans"
              dir="rtl"
            />

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600/15 hover:bg-violet-600/25 border border-violet-500/30 text-violet-300 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors">
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    className="hidden"
                    onChange={handleStoryImageUpload}
                  />
                  <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>هەڵبژاردنی وێنە لە مۆبایل</span>
                </label>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">یان پاشبنەما (Background):</span>
              </div>

              {customStoryImage ? (
                <div className="relative w-full h-28 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-violet-500 shadow-md">
                  <img src={customStoryImage} alt="Uploaded" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setCustomStoryImage(null)}
                    className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-black/75 hover:bg-black rounded-full p-1.5 text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  {[
                    { id: 'gradient-1', label: 'پاریس و ئیڤڵ', img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1502602898657-3e91760cbb34?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80' },
                    { id: 'gradient-2', label: 'سروشتێ دهۆک', img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1506744038136-46273834b3fb?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80' },
                    { id: 'gradient-3', label: 'چیاکانی زاخۆ', img: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1464822759023-fed622ff2c3b?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80' },
                  ].map((bg) => (
                    <button
                      key={bg.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedBg(bg.id)}
                      className={`relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden h-16 border-2 transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                        selectedBg === bg.id ? 'border-violet-500 scale-102 ring-2 ring-violet-500/30' : 'border-zinc-800 opacity-60 hover:opacity-90'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <img src={bg.img} alt={bg.label} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      <span className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/45 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white text-center px-1">
                        {bg.label}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!newCaption.trim() && !customStoryImage}
              className="w-full py-2.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs text-white shadow-lg shadow-violet-600/30 cursor-pointer transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>بڵاوکردنەوەی ستۆری</span>
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/95 backdrop-blur-lg flex items-center justify-center select-none"
      onMouseDown={() => setIsPaused(true)}
      onMouseUp={() => setIsPaused(false)}
      onTouchStart={() => setIsPaused(true)}
      onTouchEnd={() => setIsPaused(false)}
    >
      <div className="relative w-full h-full max-w-[420px] max-h-[860px] bg-neutral-950 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden shadow-2xl rounded-none sm:rounded-3xl border border-neutral-800/40">
        {/* Background Image / Media */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
          {currentStory.mediaType === 'image' && currentStory.mediaUrl ? (
            <img
              src={currentStory.mediaUrl}
              alt={currentStory.userName}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <div
              className={`w-full h-full bg-gradient-to-br ${
                currentStory.bgGradient || 'from-purple-900 via-indigo-950 to-black'
              }`}
            />
          )}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/20 to-black/70" />
        </div>

        {/* Top Progress Bars */}
        <div className="relative z-10 px-4 pt-3 flex gap-1">
          {stories.map((s, idx) => (
            <div
              key={s.id}
              className="flex-1 h-1 bg-white/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden"
            >
              <div
                className="h-full bg-white transition-all duration-100 ease-linear rounded-full"
                style={{
                  width:
                    idx < currentIndex
                      ? '100%'
                      : idx === currentIndex
                      ? `${progress}%`
                      : '0%',
                }}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Top Header */}
        <div className="relative z-10 px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            {currentStory.userName === 'Kurd Chat' ? (
              <KurdChatLogo size="sm" withGlow />
            ) : currentStory.userAvatar ? (
              <img
                src={currentStory.userAvatar}
                alt={currentStory.userName}
                className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ring-violet-500"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-sm">
                {currentStory.userName[0]}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-col text-right">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span>{currentStory.userName}</span>
                <BlueVerifiedBadge size="xs" />
                {currentStory.userId === currentUser.id && (
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-violet-600/80 border border-violet-400/50 text-[10px] font-bold text-white">
                    ستۆریێ تە (Your story)
                  </span>
                )}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-white/70 font-sans">
                {currentStory.timestamp}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {currentStory.userId === currentUser.id && onDeleteStory && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  if (confirm('ئایا دڵنیایت لە سڕینەوەی ئەم ستۆرییە؟')) {
                    onDeleteStory(currentStory.id);
                    onClose();
                  }
                }}
                title="سڕینەوەی ستۆری"
                className="p-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-950/70 hover:bg-rose-900 border border-rose-800 text-rose-300 backdrop-blur-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                setShowAddStory(true);
              }}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-white/20 hover:bg-white/30 text-white text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full backdrop-blur-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>ستۆری نوێ</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-full bg-black/40 text-white hover:bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation tap areas */}
        <div className="relative z-10 flex-1 flex">
          <div
            className="w-1/2 h-full cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-start pl-2 opacity-0 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
            onClick={handlePrev}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-8 h-8 text-white/50" />
          </div>
          <div
            className="w-1/2 h-full cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-end pr-2 opacity-0 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
            onClick={handleNext}
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-8 h-8 text-white/50" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Bottom Caption & Interactive Reply Bar */}
        <div className="relative z-10 p-4 pb-6 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {currentStory.caption && (
            <div
              className={`backdrop-blur-md p-4 rounded-2xl border text-center transition-all ${
                currentStory.mediaType === 'text'
                  ? 'bg-black/60 border-white/20 my-auto py-8'
                  : 'bg-black/50 border-white/10 text-right'
              }`}
              dir="rtl"
            >
              <p
                className={`font-bold leading-relaxed text-white drop-shadow-md ${
                  currentStory.mediaType === 'text'
                    ? 'text-lg sm:text-xl'
                    : 'text-sm font-medium'
                }`}
              >
                {currentStory.caption}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {currentStory.userId === currentUser.id ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md p-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-white/15" dir="rtl">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-white">
                <span className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600/20 text-rose-400">
                  <Heart className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />
                </span>
                <div className="flex flex-col text-right">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
                    {currentStory.likesCount && currentStory.likesCount > 0
                      ? `${currentStory.likesCount} کەس لایک کریە`
                      : 'هێشتا چ کەسی لایک نەکریە'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
                    ئەم ستۆرییە لەلایەن تۆوە بڵاوکراوەتەوە
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowAddStory(true)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-md"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>دانانا دانەکا دی</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2" dir="rtl">
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="وەڵامدانەوەی ستۆری..."
                className="flex-1 bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20 rounded-full px-4 py-2.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-white/70 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleToggleLike}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-full backdrop-blur-md transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  liked
                    ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white scale-105 shadow-md shadow-rose-600/30'
                    : 'bg-white/15 text-white hover:bg-white/30'
                }`}
              >
                <Heart
                  className={`w-5 h-5 ${liked ? 'fill-current' : ''}`}
                />
                {currentStory.likesCount && currentStory.likesCount > 0 ? (
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold font-sans">{currentStory.likesCount}</span>
                ) : null}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="p-2.5 rounded-full bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white backdrop-blur-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Send className="w-4 h-4 transform rotate-180" />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
📄 src/components/VoiceChatModal.tsx 526 lines 
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { User, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import { translations } from '../utils/i18n';
import {
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  PhoneOff,
  Users,
  Radio,
  Sparkles,
  Hand,
  Minimize2,
  Maximize2,
  X,
  Shield,
  Activity,
  Gift,
  Coins,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { playMicToggleSound, playVoiceChatJoinSound, playVoiceChatLeaveSound, playSendSound } from '../utils/audio';
import { VoiceRoomGiftsSheet } from './VoiceRoomGiftsSheet';
import { LiveGiftAnimationOverlay } from './LiveGiftAnimationOverlay';
import { VoiceGiftItem, LiveSentGift } from '../types/gifts';

export interface VoiceParticipant {
  user: User;
  isMuted: boolean;
  isSpeaking: boolean;
  hasHandRaised?: boolean;
  recentGift?: string;
}

interface VoiceChatModalProps {
  currentUser: User;
  contact?: User | null;
  allUsers: User[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  isOpen: boolean;
  isMinimized: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onToggleMinimize: () => void;
  onUpdateCurrentUserCoins?: (newCoins: number) => void;
  onOpenWallet?: () => void;
}

export const VoiceChatModal: React.FC<VoiceChatModalProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  contact,
  allUsers,
  dialect,
  isOpen,
  isMinimized,
  onClose,
  onToggleMinimize,
  onUpdateCurrentUserCoins,
  onOpenWallet,
}) => {
  const t = translations[dialect];
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState(false);
  const [isSpeakerOn, setIsSpeakerOn] = useState(true);
  const [hasRaisedHand, setHasRaisedHand] = useState(false);
  const [isNoiseCancelOn, setIsNoiseCancelOn] = useState(true);
  const [callDuration, setCallDuration] = useState(0);

  // Gifts & Live animations
  const [isGiftsSheetOpen, setIsGiftsSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeGiftAnimation, setActiveGiftAnimation] = useState<LiveSentGift | null>(null);

  // Initial participants in the voice chat
  const [participants, setParticipants] = useState<VoiceParticipant[]>(() => {
    const list: VoiceParticipant[] = [
      {
        user: currentUser,
        isMuted: false,
        isSpeaking: true,
      },
    ];

    if (contact && contact.id !== currentUser.id) {
      list.push({
        user: contact,
        isMuted: false,
        isSpeaking: false,
      });
    } else {
      // Add a couple of active community friends
      const others = allUsers.filter((u) => u.id !== currentUser.id).slice(0, 3);
      for (const u of others) {
        list.push({
          user: u,
          isMuted: Math.random() > 0.6,
          isSpeaking: false,
        });
      }
    }
    return list;
  });

  // Play join chime on open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      playVoiceChatJoinSound();
      setCallDuration(0);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Duration counter
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setCallDuration((prev) => prev + 1);
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Dynamic speaking simulator among other participants
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const speakTimer = setInterval(() => {
      setParticipants((prev) =>
        prev.map((p) => {
          if (p.user.id === currentUser.id) {
            return { ...p, isMuted, isSpeaking: !isMuted && Math.random() > 0.3 };
          }
          if (p.isMuted) return { ...p, isSpeaking: false };
          return {
            ...p,
            isSpeaking: Math.random() > 0.55,
          };
        })
      );
    }, 1200);

    return () => clearInterval(speakTimer);
  }, [isOpen, isMuted, currentUser.id]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleToggleMute = () => {
    const nextMuted = !isMuted;
    setIsMuted(nextMuted);
    playMicToggleSound(nextMuted);
    setParticipants((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.user.id === currentUser.id ? { ...p, isMuted: nextMuted, isSpeaking: !nextMuted } : p))
    );
  };

  const handleToggleSpeaker = () => {
    setIsSpeakerOn((prev) => !prev);
  };

  const handleToggleHand = () => {
    const nextHand = !hasRaisedHand;
    setHasRaisedHand(nextHand);
    setParticipants((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.user.id === currentUser.id ? { ...p, hasHandRaised: nextHand } : p))
    );
  };

  const handleLeaveVoiceChat = () => {
    playVoiceChatLeaveSound();
    onClose();
  };

  const handleSendGift = async (recipient: User, gift: VoiceGiftItem, comboCount: number) => {
    const totalCost = gift.coins * comboCount;
    if ((currentUser.coins ?? 100) < totalCost) return;

    const newCoins = Math.max(0, (currentUser.coins ?? 100) - totalCost);
    if (onUpdateCurrentUserCoins) {
      onUpdateCurrentUserCoins(newCoins);
    }

    // Trigger visual celebration animation
    setActiveGiftAnimation({
      id: `live-gift-${Date.now()}`,
      senderId: currentUser.id,
      senderName: currentUser.name,
      senderAvatar: currentUser.avatar,
      recipientId: recipient.id,
      recipientName: recipient.name,
      gift,
      comboCount,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    });

    // Mark recipient with recent gift icon
    setParticipants((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.user.id === recipient.id ? { ...p, recentGift: gift.icon } : p))
    );

    playSendSound();
    setIsGiftsSheetOpen(false);

    // Persist in background
    try {
      await fetch('/api/wallet/transfer', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          senderId: currentUser.id,
          recipientId: recipient.id,
          amount: totalCost,
          note: `Live Voice Gift: ${gift.name} x${comboCount}`,
        }),
      });
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleRechargeCoins = (amount: number) => {
    const newCoins = (currentUser.coins ?? 100) + amount;
    if (onUpdateCurrentUserCoins) {
      onUpdateCurrentUserCoins(newCoins);
    }
    // Background recharge
    fetch('/api/wallet/recharge', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        userId: currentUser.id,
        packageAmount: amount,
        paymentMethod: 'SuperQi/FIB/FastPay/NassPay (7502236113)',
      }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  };

  const formatDuration = (totalSec: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(totalSec / 60);
    const secs = totalSec % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  // Minimized Floating Pill Mode
  if (isMinimized) {
    return (
      <div
        className="fixed bottom-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 bg-[#121218]/95 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2.5 shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-3 text-zinc-100 select-none animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-3"
        dir="rtl"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
          <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-violet-400" />
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
            {contact ? `دەنگەچاتی: ${contact.name}` : 'دەنگەچاتی گشتی'}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-400">
            {formatDuration(callDuration)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-r border-zinc-800 pr-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleMute}
            className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer ${
              isMuted
                ? 'bg-rose-950/80 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800'
                : 'bg-emerald-950/80 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800'
            }`}
          >
            {isMuted ? <MicOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Mic className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleMinimize}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLeaveVoiceChat}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-sm"
          >
            <PhoneOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center p-3 select-none" dir="rtl">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-[#0e0e14] border border-zinc-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col relative text-zinc-100 max-h-[92vh] overflow-hidden">
        {/* Ambient background glow */}
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-1/4 w-64 h-64 bg-violet-900/20 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-1/4 w-64 h-64 bg-emerald-900/15 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Top Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3.5 border-b border-zinc-800/80 relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white shadow-lg shadow-violet-600/30">
              <Radio className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                  {contact ? `دەنگەچات لەگەڵ ${contact.name}` : 'ژوورا دەنگەچاتێ'}
                </h3>
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-ping" />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                <span className="text-emerald-400 font-mono font-bold">{formatDuration(callDuration)}</span>
                <span>·</span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Users className="w-3 h-3 text-zinc-400" />
                  <span>{participants.length} بەژداربوو</span>
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsGiftsSheetOpen(true)}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 hover:bg-amber-500/25 border border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer transition-all shadow-xs"
              title="باڵانسی کۆین و دیارییەکان"
            >
              <Coins className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="font-mono">{(currentUser.coins ?? 100).toLocaleString()}</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onToggleMinimize}
              title="کەمکردنەوە بۆ لای خوارەوە"
              className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer border border-zinc-800"
            >
              <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4" />

              title="چوونەدەرەوە لە دەنگەچات"
              className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-rose-950/80 hover:bg-rose-900 text-rose-300 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer border border-rose-900/80"
            >

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-violet-400 animate-pulse" />
            <span>شەپۆلێن دەنگی ڕاستەوخۆ</span>

                      <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                      <span>قسە دەکات...</span>

          {/* Mute/Unmute Mic Button */}
          <button
            type="button"

            className={`flex-1 py-3 px-4 rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-md active:scale-95 ${
              isMuted
                ? 'bg-rose-950/80 hover:bg-rose-900 border border-rose-800 text-rose-200'

            <span>{isMuted ? 'مایکرۆفۆن داخراوە' : 'مایکرۆفۆن کراوەیە'}</span>

          >
            <Hand className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>

          {/* TikTok Live Gifts Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsGiftsSheetOpen(true)}
            title="دیارییەکانی لایڤ (Live Gifts)"
            className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-rose-600 via-pink-600 to-rose-500 hover:from-rose-500 hover:to-pink-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center border border-pink-400/40 shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 animate-pulse"
          >
            <Gift className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />
          </button>

          {/* Speaker Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleToggleSpeaker}
            title="دەنگبێژ / بڵندگۆ"
            className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center border transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 ${
              isSpeakerOn
                ? 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 text-violet-400 hover:bg-zinc-800'
                : 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-800 text-zinc-500'
            }`}
          >
            {isSpeakerOn ? <Volume2 className="w-5 h-5" /> : <VolumeX className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>

          {/* End/Leave Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleLeaveVoiceChat}
            title="دەرچوون لە دەنگەچات"
            className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 active:scale-95"
          >
            <PhoneOff className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TIKTOK STYLE GIFTS BOTTOM SHEET */}
      <VoiceRoomGiftsSheet
        currentUser={currentUser}
        participants={participants}
        dialect={dialect}
        isOpen={isGiftsSheetOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsGiftsSheetOpen(false)}
        onSendGift={handleSendGift}
        onRechargeCoins={handleRechargeCoins}
      />

      {/* LIVE GIFT FLYING ANIMATION OVERLAY */}
      <LiveGiftAnimationOverlay
        activeGift={activeGiftAnimation}
        onAnimationEnd={() => setActiveGiftAnimation(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { User, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import {
  GiftCategory,
  VoiceGiftItem,
  VoiceSongItem,
  RECHARGE_PACKAGES,
  RechargePackage,
} from '../types/gifts';
import { VOICE_GIFTS_CATALOG, VOICE_SONGS_CATALOG } from '../data/voiceGiftsData';
import { VoiceParticipant } from './VoiceChatModal';
import {
  X,
  Coins,
  Sparkles,
  ChevronRight,
  Disc3,
  Copy,
  Check,
  CreditCard,
  Building2,
  Smartphone,
  Wallet,
  Play,
  Flame,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface VoiceRoomGiftsSheetProps {
  currentUser: User;
  participants: VoiceParticipant[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSendGift: (recipient: User, gift: VoiceGiftItem, comboCount: number) => void;
  onRechargeCoins: (amount: number) => void;
}

const OFFICIAL_PAYMENTS = [
  { id: 'superqi', name: 'SuperQi', account: '7502236113', icon: Smartphone },
  { id: 'fib', name: 'First Iraqi Bank (FIB)', account: '7502236113', icon: Building2 },
  { id: 'nasspay', name: 'NassPay', account: '7502236113', icon: CreditCard },
  { id: 'fastpay', name: 'FastPay', account: '7502236113', icon: Smartphone },
  { id: 'usdt', name: 'USDT TRC20', account: 'TAVaLZGavKH5ZaSiRY3mc65Eji6DPsXrRR', icon: Wallet },
];

export const VoiceRoomGiftsSheet: React.FC<VoiceRoomGiftsSheetProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  participants,
  dialect,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSendGift,
  onRechargeCoins,
}) => {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<GiftCategory>('gifts');
  const [selectedGift, setSelectedGift] = useState<VoiceGiftItem>(VOICE_GIFTS_CATALOG[0]);
  const [selectedSong, setSelectedSong] = useState<VoiceSongItem | null>(null);
  const [selectedRecipientId, setSelectedRecipientId] = useState<string>(() => {
    // Default to first other participant on mic
    const other = participants.find((p) => p.user.id !== currentUser.id);
    return other ? other.user.id : currentUser.id;
  });
  const [comboCount, setComboCount] = useState<number>(1);
  const [showRechargeSheet, setShowRechargeSheet] = useState(false);
  const [selectedRechargePack, setSelectedRechargePack] = useState<RechargePackage | null>(null);
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const userCoins = currentUser.coins ?? 100;

  // Filter gifts by active category
  const displayedGifts = VOICE_GIFTS_CATALOG.filter((g) => g.category === activeCategory);

  const selectedRecipient =
    participants.find((p) => p.user.id === selectedRecipientId)?.user || currentUser;

  const totalCost = selectedGift ? selectedGift.coins * comboCount : 0;
  const canAfford = userCoins >= totalCost;

  const handleCopy = (id: string, text: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2000);
  };

  const handleSend = () => {
    if (!canAfford) {
      setShowRechargeSheet(true);
      return;
    }
    if (activeCategory === 'songs' && selectedSong) {
      // Send song as special gift
      const songAsGift: VoiceGiftItem = {
        id: selectedSong.id,
        name: selectedSong.title,
        kurName: `گۆرانی: ${selectedSong.title}`,
        category: 'songs',
        coins: selectedSong.coins,
        icon: '🎵',
        animationType: 'flight',
      };
      onSendGift(selectedRecipient, songAsGift, 1);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedGift) {
      onSendGift(selectedRecipient, selectedGift, comboCount);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickRechargeConfirm = (pkg: RechargePackage) => {
    onRechargeCoins(pkg.coins);
    setShowRechargeSheet(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex flex-col justify-end select-none animate-in fade-in duration-200" dir="rtl">
      {/* Tap outside to close */}
      <div className="flex-1" onClick={onClose} />

      {/* Main Bottom Sheet Container */}
      <div className="w-full bg-[#11121a]/98 border-t border-zinc-800 rounded-t-[32px] p-4 shadow-2xl flex flex-col max-h-[80vh] relative text-white overflow-hidden">
        {/* Decorative Pull Notch */}
        <div className="w-10 h-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-700/80 mx-auto mb-3" />

        {/* Top Header: Recipient Selector & Close */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800/80">
          {/* Recipient Selector */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">ناردن بۆ:</span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar max-w-[240px]">
              {participants.map((p) => {
                const isSelected = p.user.id === selectedRecipientId;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={p.user.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedRecipientId(p.user.id)}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-600 to-pink-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-rose-600/30 ring-1 ring-rose-400'
                        : 'bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 hover:text-white'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <img
                      src={p.user.avatar || `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${p.user.id}`}
                      alt={p.user.name}
                      className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full object-cover"
                    />
                    <span className="truncate max-w-[80px]">
                      {p.user.id === currentUser.id ? 'هەمووان' : p.user.name}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Close sheet */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Categories Tabs (TikTok Live exact: Gifts, Songs, Interactive, Exclusive) */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-2.5 border-b border-zinc-850 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
          {[
            { id: 'gifts', label: 'Gifts (دیارییەکان)' },
            { id: 'songs', label: 'Songs (گۆرانییەکان)' },
            { id: 'interactive', label: 'Interactive (تفاعلی)' },
            { id: 'exclusive', label: 'Exclusive (تایبەت)' },
          ].map((cat) => {
            const isActive = activeCategory === cat.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat.id as GiftCategory)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer shrink-0 ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 to-pink-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-rose-500/20'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                }`}
              >
                {cat.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* GIFTS GRID (4 cols x 2 rows / scrollable) */}
        {activeCategory !== 'songs' ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2.5 py-3 overflow-y-auto max-h-[38vh] no-scrollbar">
            {displayedGifts.map((gift) => {
              const isSelected = selectedGift?.id === gift.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={gift.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedGift(gift)}
                  className={`p-2 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-between text-center cursor-pointer transition-all relative group ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-rose-950/40 to-pink-950/20 border-2 border-rose-500 shadow-lg shadow-rose-500/20 scale-102'
                      : 'bg-[#161722]/60 hover:bg-[#1a1b28] border border-zinc-800/80 hover:border-zinc-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* Badge (HOT, VIP, etc) */}
                  {gift.badge && (
                    <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 px-1 py-0.2 rounded-md bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 text-[8px] font-black text-black uppercase shadow-xs">
                      {gift.badge}
                    </span>
                  )}

                  {/* 3D Animated Icon */}
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl group-hover:scale-115 transition-transform duration-200 my-1">
                    {gift.icon}
                  </div>

                  {/* Name */}
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-100 truncate w-full">
                    {gift.name}
                  </span>

                  {/* Price */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-amber-400 mt-0.5">
                    <Coins className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>{gift.coins.toLocaleString()}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* SONGS VINYL RECORD TAB */
          <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2.5 py-3 overflow-y-auto max-h-[38vh] no-scrollbar">
            {VOICE_SONGS_CATALOG.map((song) => {
              const isSelected = selectedSong?.id === song.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={song.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedSong(song)}
                  className={`p-2.5 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-between text-center cursor-pointer transition-all relative ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-b from-emerald-950/40 to-teal-950/20 border-2 border-emerald-500 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20'
                      : 'bg-[#161722]/60 hover:bg-[#1a1b28] border border-zinc-800/80'
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* Rotating Vinyl Record */}
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr ${song.color} border-2 border-zinc-900 shadow-md flex items-center justify-center text-white my-1 ${isSelected ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}>
                    <Disc3 className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </div>

                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-100 truncate w-full">
                    {song.title}
                  </span>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-amber-400 mt-0.5">
                    <Coins className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>{song.coins}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* BOTTOM ACTION BAR: RECHARGE & SEND WITH COMBO */}
        <div className="pt-3 border-t border-zinc-800/80 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mt-1">
          {/* Left: Recharge Button & Balance Pill */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowRechargeSheet(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900/90 hover:bg-zinc-850 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-xs active:scale-95"
          >
            <Coins className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span className="font-mono text-amber-300">{userCoins.toLocaleString()}</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">شحن باڵانس</span>
            <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-400 rotate-180" />
          </button>

          {/* Right: Combo multiplier + Send button */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {/* Combo selector */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-zinc-900/80 p-0.5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800">
              {[1, 5, 10, 99].map((count) => (
                <button
                  key={count}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setComboCount(count)}
                  className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-black transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                    comboCount === count
                      ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40'
                      : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  x{count}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Main Send Button */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSend}
              className={`px-6 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-black text-xs transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-lg active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                canAfford
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-500 via-pink-500 to-rose-600 hover:from-rose-400 hover:to-pink-500 text-white shadow-rose-500/30'
                  : 'bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-amber-500/20'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{canAfford ? 'ناردن (Send)' : 'پڕکردنەوە'}</span>
              <Coins className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="font-mono">{totalCost > 0 ? totalCost.toLocaleString() : ''}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* TIKTOK STYLE RECHARGE OVERLAY MODAL */}
        {showRechargeSheet && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[#0e0f17] z-50 p-4 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Coins className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">پڕکردنەوەی باڵانسی کۆین</h4>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400">باڵانسی ئێستا: {userCoins.toLocaleString()} کۆین</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowRechargeSheet(false)}
                className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Official Payment Accounts Reminder */}
            <div className="my-2 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#141520] border border-zinc-800 space-y-2">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>حسابات الدفع العراقية المعتمدة (کۆپیکردن):</span>
              </span>

              <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
                {OFFICIAL_PAYMENTS.map((m) => {
                  const isCopied = copiedId === m.id;
                  const Icon = m.icon;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={m.id}
                      className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                        <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-white truncate">{m.name}:</span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold select-all truncate">
                          {m.account}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleCopy(m.id, m.account)}
                        className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {isCopied ? <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3 h-3" />}
                        <span>{isCopied ? 'کۆپی کرا' : 'کۆپی'}</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* TikTok Recharge Packages Grid */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-300">پاکێجی دیاریکراو هەڵبژێرە:</span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {RECHARGE_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedRechargePack?.coins === pkg.coins;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={pkg.coins}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedRechargePack(pkg)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border transition-all cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-amber-500/15 border-amber-500 text-white shadow-md'
                          : 'bg-[#151622] border-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold font-mono text-sm text-white">
                          <Coins className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                          <span>{pkg.coins.toLocaleString()}</span>
                        </div>
                        {pkg.popular && (
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-md bg-rose-600 text-[9px] font-black text-white">
                            باو
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-400 font-mono mt-1">
                        {pkg.iqdPrice}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Confirm Recharge Button */}
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-zinc-800 flex gap-2 mt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={!selectedRechargePack}
                onClick={() => selectedRechargePack && handleQuickRechargeConfirm(selectedRechargePack)}
                className="flex-1 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-600 to-pink-600 hover:from-rose-500 hover:to-pink-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-white font-black text-xs rounded-2xl transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30"
              >
                {selectedRechargePack
                  ? `شحن +${selectedRechargePack.coins} کۆین (${selectedRechargePack.iqdPrice})`
                  : 'پاکێجێک دیاری بکە'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
📄 src/components/CoinWalletModal.tsx 838 lines 
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { User, Dialect, CoinTransaction } from '../types/chat';
import {
  X,
  Coins,
  Gift,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowUpRight,
  ArrowDownLeft,
  Check,
  Plus,
  Clock,
  Send,
  AlertCircle,
  TrendingUp,
  Copy,
  CreditCard,
  Building2,
  Smartphone,
  Wallet,
  ExternalLink,
  ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface CoinWalletModalProps {
  currentUser: User;
  allUsers: User[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdateCurrentUserCoins: (newCoins: number) => void;
}

interface PaymentMethod {

  name: string;
  kurName: string;
  account: string;
  type: 'wallet' | 'bank' | 'card' | 'crypto';
  badge: string;

  color: string;
}

export interface RechargePackage {
  amount: number;
  priceUsd: string;
  priceIqd: string;
  label: string;
  desc: string;
  popular?: boolean;
}

export const RECHARGE_PACKAGES: RechargePackage[] = [
  { amount: 100, priceUsd: '$0.99', priceIqd: '١,٥٠٠ دینار', label: '١٠٠ کۆین', desc: 'دیاری خێرا' },
