export const SettingsModal: React.FC<SettingsModalProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  dialect,
  soundEnabled,
  onUpdateUser,
  onDialectChange,
  onToggleSound,
  onOpenAdmin,
  onOpenWallet,
  onLogout,
  onClose,
  isInline = false,
}) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState(currentUser.name);
  const [bio, setBio] = useState(currentUser.bio || '');
  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(currentUser.avatar || '');
  const [avatarTab, setAvatarTab] = useState<'upload' | 'presets' | 'url'>('upload');
  const [customUrl, setCustomUrl] = useState('');
  const [savedNotice, setSavedNotice] = useState(false);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const t = translations[dialect];

  // Upload photo from device / camera
  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const dataUrl = event.target?.result as string;
      if (dataUrl) {
        setAvatar(dataUrl);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleApplyCustomUrl = () => {
    if (customUrl.trim().startsWith('http')) {
      setAvatar(customUrl.trim());
      setCustomUrl('');
    }
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    onUpdateUser({
      name: name.trim(),
      bio: bio.trim(),
      avatar: avatar || currentUser.avatar,
    });
    setSavedNotice(true);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setSavedNotice(false);
      if (onClose) onClose();
    }, 1200);
  };

  const content = (
    <div className={`w-full ${isInline ? 'h-full max-w-md mx-auto p-4' : 'max-w-[400px] bg-[#18181c] border border-neutral-800 rounded-[32px] p-5 shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh]'} flex flex-col gap-4 text-white overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar`} dir="rtl">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-neutral-800/80 pb-3">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-base flex items-center gap-2">
          <UserIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
          <span>{t.settings}</span>
        </h3>
        {!isInline && onClose && (
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-full text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

        {/* Profile Card & Avatar Editor */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSaveProfile} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          {/* Avatar Preview & Direct Upload Area */}
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-neutral-900/90 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800/80 text-center relative overflow-hidden">
            <div className="relative group cursor-pointer" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}>
              {avatar ? (
                <img
                  src={avatar}
                  alt={currentUser.name}
                  className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full object-cover ring-4 ring-purple-600/60 shadow-xl group-hover:opacity-85 transition-opacity"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-blue-600 font-extrabold text-3xl flex items-center justify-center text-white ring-4 ring-blue-500/60 shadow-xl">
                  {currentUser.name[0]}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Floating Camera Button */}
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 rounded-full opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity">
                <Camera className="w-7 h-7 text-white drop-shadow-md" />
              </div>

              <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 p-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 rounded-full text-white shadow-md border-2 border-[#18181c]">
                <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <input
              type="file"
              ref={fileInputRef}
              accept="image/*"
              className="hidden"
              onChange={handleFileUpload}
            />

            <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{name || currentUser.name}</span>
                <UserBadges user={currentUser} size="xs" />
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400 font-mono">@{currentUser.username}</span>
            </div>

            {/* Avatar Selection Tabs */}
            <div className="w-full pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800/80 flex flex-col gap-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-300">
                <span className="text-purple-400">گۆڕینی وێنەی پڕۆفایل / تعديل الأفاتار</span>
                <span>📸</span>
              </div>

              {/* Pill tabs: Upload, Presets, URL */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-neutral-950 p-1 rounded-xl text-xs">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                  className="flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-600/30 hover:bg-blue-600/50 text-blue-300 border border-blue-500/30 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>وێنە لە مۆبایل</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAvatarTab('presets')}
                  className={`flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg transition-all cursor-pointer font-semibold ${
                    avatarTab === 'presets'
                      ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white'
                      : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  ئەڤەتاری ئامادە
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAvatarTab('url')}
                  className={`flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded-lg transition-all cursor-pointer font-semibold ${
                    avatarTab === 'url'
                      ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white'
                      : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  بەستەر / Link
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Preset Avatars Carousel */}
              {avatarTab === 'presets' && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar p-1">
                    {AVATAR_PRESETS.map((p) => {
                      const isSelected = avatar === p.url;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={p.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setAvatar(p.url)}
                          className={`relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden aspect-square border-2 transition-all cursor-pointer group ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'border-blue-500 ring-2 ring-blue-500/50 scale-105'
                              : 'border-neutral-800 hover:border-neutral-600'
                          }`}
                          title={p.name}
                        >
                          <img
                            src={p.url}
                            alt={p.name}
                            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                          />
                          <span className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 bg-black/70 text-[9px] font-bold text-white py-0.5 text-center truncate px-0.5">
                            {p.tag}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* URL Input */}
              {avatarTab === 'url' && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" dir="ltr">
                  <input
                    type="url"
                    placeholder="https://example.com/avatar.jpg"
                    value={customUrl}
                    onChange={(e) => setCustomUrl(e.target.value)}
                    className="flex-1 bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-700/80 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-neutral-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleApplyCustomUrl}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                  >
                    دانان
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Name Field */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-right">
            <label className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 font-medium">ناو / الاسم:</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="bg-[#232328] border border-neutral-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 font-medium"
              dir="rtl"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Bio Field */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-right">
            <label className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 font-medium">بایۆ / دەربارەی من:</label>
            <textarea
              value={bio}
              onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="دەربارەی خۆت بنووسە..."
              rows={2}
              className="bg-[#232328] border border-neutral-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500 resize-none font-medium"
              dir="rtl"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit Button */}
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="w-full py-3 bg-[#0084ff] hover:bg-[#0073e6] active:scale-98 text-xs font-bold rounded-2xl transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/25"
          >
            {savedNotice ? (
              <>
                <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
                <span>پاشەکەوت کرا! (تم الحفظ)</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span>پاشەکەوتکردنی وێنە و زانیارییەکان 💾</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* Coin Balance Card */}
        <div className="p-3.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/10 via-amber-500/5 to-zinc-900/60 rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-between shadow-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-600 to-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-950 shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20">
              <Coins className="w-5 h-5 text-zinc-950" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-300">
                {dialect === 'english' ? 'Coin Balance' : dialect === 'badini' ? 'باڵانسی کۆینان' : 'باڵانسی کۆین'}
              </span>
              <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-white tracking-tight">
                {(currentUser.coins ?? 100).toLocaleString()}{' '}
                <span className="text-xs text-amber-400 font-medium">کۆین</span>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          {onOpenWallet && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if (onClose && !isInline) onClose();
                onOpenWallet();
              }}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-zinc-950 text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer active:scale-95"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{dialect === 'english' ? 'Wallet' : dialect === 'badini' ? 'جزدان' : 'جزدان'}</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Preferences Section */}
        <div className="pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800/80 flex flex-col gap-3">
          {/* Language Selection */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs" dir="rtl">
            <span className="text-neutral-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
              <span>زمان / اللغات:</span>
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#232328] p-1 rounded-xl">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onDialectChange('sorani')}
                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-xs transition-all ${
                  dialect === 'sorani' ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold' : 'text-neutral-400'
                }`}
              >
                سۆرانی
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onDialectChange('badini')}
                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-xs transition-all ${
                  dialect === 'badini' ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold' : 'text-neutral-400'
                }`}
              >
                بادینی
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onDialectChange('english')}
                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-xs transition-all ${
                  dialect === 'english' ? 'bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold' : 'text-neutral-400'
                }`}
              >
                EN
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Sound Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs" dir="rtl">
            <span className="text-neutral-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>دەنگی پەیامەکان:</span>
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onToggleSound}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                soundEnabled
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                  : 'bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-400'
              }`}
            >
              {soundEnabled ? 'چالاکە 🔊' : 'بێدەنگ 🔇'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Admin Panel button if admin */}
          {(currentUser.isAdmin || currentUser.username === 'sabah_sndi') && onOpenAdmin && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if (onClose) onClose();
                onOpenAdmin();
              }}
              className="w-full py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950/80 via-purple-950/80 to-emerald-950/80 hover:border-amber-400 border border-amber-500/50 rounded-2xl text-amber-300 font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer transition-all"
            >
              <KurdistanAdminVerifyBadge size="sm" />
              <span>کردنەوەی Admin Panel</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Download Full Source Code Document */}
          <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#161620] border border-amber-500/30 flex flex-col gap-2">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>فایلی سەرجەم کۆدەکانی پڕۆژە (Source Code Export)</span>
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <a
                href="/KurdChat_Domino_Source_Code.pdf"
                download="KurdChat_Domino_Source_Code.pdf"
                className="py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-500/40 text-amber-300 text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all text-center cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>داگرتن وەک PDF</span>
              </a>
              <a
                href="/source-code.html"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noreferrer"
                className="py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/30 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all text-center cursor-pointer"
              >
                <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>بینین و چاپ (HTML)</span>
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Logout */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onLogout}
            className="w-full py-2.5 bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 border border-neutral-800 text-red-400 font-semibold text-xs rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>چوونەدەرەوە (Log out)</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
  );

  if (isInline) {
    return content;
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-3 select-none">
      {content}
    </div>
  );
};
📄 src/components/NewChatModal.tsx 153 lines 
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { User, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import { translations } from '../utils/i18n';
import { X, Search, UserPlus, Share2, Check, UserCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { KurdistanAdminVerifyBadge, UserBadges } from './KurdistanBadge';

interface NewChatModalProps {
  currentUser: User;
  users: User[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSelectUser: (user: User) => void;
}

export const NewChatModal: React.FC<NewChatModalProps> = ({
  currentUser,
  users,
  dialect,
  onClose,
  onSelectUser,
}) => {
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const t = translations[dialect];

  const filteredUsers = users.filter(
    (u) =>
      u.id !== currentUser.id &&
      (u.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        u.username.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()))
  );

  const handleCopyLink = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.origin);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#18181c] border border-neutral-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-4 text-white">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-neutral-800/80 pb-3">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-base flex items-center gap-2">
            <UserPlus className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-500" />
            {t.newChat}
          </h3>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-full text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Search */}
        <div className="relative">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder={t.searchPlaceholder}
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-[#232328] text-white placeholder-neutral-400 pl-4 pr-10 py-3 rounded-2xl text-xs border border-neutral-700/60 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none"
            dir={dialect === 'english' ? 'ltr' : 'rtl'}
          />
          <Search className="absolute right-3.5 top-3.5 w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 pointer-events-none" />
        </div>

        {/* Registered Users list */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1 text-right">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-400">
              {t.users} ({filteredUsers.length}):
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400">بەردەست لەسەر هێڵ 🟢</span>
          </div>

          {filteredUsers.length > 0 ? (
            filteredUsers.map((user) => (
              <button
                key={user.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectUser(user)}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-2xl hover:bg-neutral-800/80 transition-all text-right cursor-pointer group"
                dir="rtl"
              >
                <div className="relative shrink-0">
                  {user.avatar ? (
                    <img
                      src={user.avatar}
                      alt={user.name}
                      className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover ring-1 ring-neutral-700 group-hover:ring-blue-500 transition-all"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                      {user.name[0]}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {user.isOnline !== false && (
                    <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-emerald-500 border-2 border-[#18181c] rounded-full" />
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-white truncate">
                      {user.name}

                    <UserBadges user={user} size="xs" />

                  {user.bio && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 truncate mt-0.5">
                      {user.bio}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </button>
            ))
          ) : (
            <div className="py-6 px-3 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center gap-3 bg-neutral-900/40 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800/60">
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">

              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleCopyLink}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-all"
              >
                {copied ? (
                  <>
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4" />

                    <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    کۆپیکردنی لینکی ئەپ
                  </>

      </div>
    </div>
  );

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AppNotification, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import { translations } from '../utils/i18n';
import {
  X,
  Bell,
  Heart,
  UserPlus,
  UserCheck,
  MessageSquare,
  Megaphone,
  Coins,
  CheckCheck,
  Trash2,
  ChevronLeft,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationsModalProps {
  notifications: AppNotification[];
  dialect: Dialect;
  onClose: () => void;
  onMarkAsRead: (notificationId?: string) => void;
  onClearNotifications: () => void;
  onSelectNotificationAction?: (notification: AppNotification) => void;
  onSendTestNotification?: (type: 'follow_request' | 'story_like' | 'message' | 'coin_gift') => void;
}

export const NotificationsModal: React.FC<NotificationsModalProps> = ({
  notifications,
  dialect,
  onClose,
  onMarkAsRead,
  onClearNotifications,
  onSelectNotificationAction,
  onSendTestNotification,
}) => {

  const [showTestMenu, setShowTestMenu] = useState(false);
  const t = translations[dialect];

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  const filteredNotifications = notifications.filter((n) => {
    if (activeFilter === 'unread') return !n.read;
    if (activeFilter === 'messages') return n.type === 'message';
    if (activeFilter === 'follows') return n.type === 'follow_request' || n.type === 'follow_accept';
    if (activeFilter === 'likes') return n.type === 'story_like';
    if (activeFilter === 'coins') return n.type === 'coin_gift';
    return true;
  });

  const getIconForType = (type: AppNotification['type']) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'story_like':
        return <Heart className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400 fill-current" />;
      case 'follow_request':
        return <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-violet-400" />;
      case 'follow_accept':
        return <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />;
      case 'coin_gift':
        return <Coins className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />;
      case 'message':
        return <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400" />;
      case 'broadcast':
        return <Megaphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />;
      default:
        return <Bell className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-400" />;
    }

    switch (type) {
      case 'story_like':
        return 'bg-rose-950/80 border-rose-800/80';
      case 'follow_request':
        return 'bg-violet-950/80 border-violet-800/80';
      case 'follow_accept':
        return 'bg-emerald-950/80 border-emerald-800/80';
      case 'coin_gift':
        return 'bg-amber-950/80 border-amber-800/80';
      case 'message':
        return 'bg-blue-950/80 border-blue-800/80';
      case 'broadcast':
        return 'bg-amber-950/80 border-amber-700/80';

        return 'bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-700';

            </div>

              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>{dialect === 'english' ? 'Notifications' : dialect === 'badini' ? 'ئاگەهداری' : 'ئاگادارییەکان'}</span>
                {unreadCount > 0 && (

📄 src/components/NotificationToast.tsx 171 lines 
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { AppNotification, Dialect } from '../types/chat';
import {
  Heart,
  UserPlus,
  UserCheck,
  MessageSquare,
  Coins,
  Megaphone,
  Bell,
  X,
  ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface NotificationToastProps {
  notification: AppNotification | null;
  dialect: Dialect;
  onClose: () => void;
  onOpenNotification: (notification: AppNotification) => void;
}

export const NotificationToast: React.FC<NotificationToastProps> = ({
  notification,
  dialect,
  onClose,
  onOpenNotification,
}) => {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(100);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!notification) return;

    setProgress(100);
    const duration = 4800;
    const interval = 50;
    const decrement = (interval / duration) * 100;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress((prev) => {
        if (prev <= decrement) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          onClose();
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - decrement;
      });
    }, interval);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [notification?.id, onClose]);

  if (!notification) return null;

  const getActionIcon = () => {
    switch (notification.type) {
      case 'story_like':
        return <Heart className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400 fill-current" />;
      case 'follow_request':
        return <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-violet-400" />;
      case 'follow_accept':
        return <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />;
      case 'coin_gift':
        return <Coins className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />;
      case 'broadcast':
        return <Megaphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />;
      case 'message':
      default:
        return <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-violet-400" />;
    }
  };

  const getBadgeStyle = () => {
    switch (notification.type) {
      case 'story_like':
        return 'bg-rose-950/90 border-rose-700/80 text-rose-300';
      case 'follow_request':
        return 'bg-violet-950/90 border-violet-700/80 text-violet-300';
      case 'follow_accept':
        return 'bg-emerald-950/90 border-emerald-700/80 text-emerald-300';
      case 'coin_gift':
        return 'bg-amber-950/90 border-amber-700/80 text-amber-300';
      case 'broadcast':
        return 'bg-amber-950/90 border-amber-600/80 text-amber-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-violet-950/90 border-violet-700/80 text-violet-300';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 w-[92%] max-w-md pointer-events-auto transition-all animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-4 duration-300 select-none"
      dir="rtl"
    >

            style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="relative shrink-0">
          <img

              notification.actorAvatar ||
              `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${notification.actorId || 'kurd'}`
            }

📄 src/components/LiveGiftAnimationOverlay.tsx 100 lines 
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { LiveSentGift } from '../types/gifts';
import { Sparkles, Flame, Crown } from 'lucide-react';

interface LiveGiftAnimationOverlayProps {
  activeGift: LiveSentGift | null;
  onAnimationEnd: () => void;
}

export const LiveGiftAnimationOverlay: React.FC<LiveGiftAnimationOverlayProps> = ({
  activeGift,
  onAnimationEnd,
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeGift) return;
    const duration = activeGift.gift.coins >= 10000 ? 4500 : 3200;
    const timer = setTimeout(onAnimationEnd, duration);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [activeGift, onAnimationEnd]);

  if (!activeGift) return null;

  const isEpic = activeGift.gift.coins >= 10000;
  const isHigh = activeGift.gift.coins >= 200;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-hidden" dir="rtl">
      {/* EPIC FULL-SCREEN BACKGROUND LIGHT BURST */}
      {isEpic && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center animate-in fade-in duration-500">
          <div className="w-[500px] h-[500px] rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-500/20 via-rose-500/20 to-violet-500/20 blur-3xl animate-pulse" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(#f59e0b_2px,transparent_2px)] [background-size:32px_32px] opacity-40 animate-ping" />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TIKTOK STYLE GIFT BANNER (Slides in from right/center) */}
      <div className="relative animate-in slide-in-from-right-8 fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-300 max-w-md w-full flex flex-col items-center">
        {/* Top Royal Crown for Big Gifts */}
        {isEpic && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-rose-500 text-black font-black text-xs shadow-xl animate-bounce mb-2">
            <Crown className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />
            <span>دیاری پاشایەتی • ROYAL GIFT</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* The Card Banner */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5 pr-4 pl-6 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-black/90 via-zinc-900/95 to-black/90 border-2 border-rose-500/60 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl relative">
          {/* Sender Avatar */}
          <div className="relative shrink-0">
            <img
              src={activeGift.senderAvatar || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1534528741775-53994a69daeb?w=150'}
              alt={activeGift.senderName}
              className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ring-rose-500 shadow-md"
            />
            <span className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-rose-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold">
              ★
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Text Details */}
          <div className="flex flex-col text-right min-w-[130px]">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate max-w-[150px]">
              {activeGift.senderName}
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-300">
              <span className="text-rose-400 font-bold">پێشکەشی کرد بە:</span>
              <span className="text-amber-300 font-bold truncate max-w-[90px]">
                {activeGift.recipientName}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-medium">
              {activeGift.gift.kurName}
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Big 3D Gift Icon */}
          <div className="text-4xl shrink-0 animate-bounce transition-transform duration-300 scale-120 drop-shadow-[0_10px_20px_rgba(244,63,94,0.4)]">
            {activeGift.gift.icon}
          </div>

          {/* Combo Multiplier Pill */}
          <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-600 to-amber-500 text-white font-black text-sm px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shadow-lg italic animate-pulse">
            <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
            <span>x{activeGift.comboCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Floating Sparks particles */}
        <div className="flex gap-4 mt-2">
          <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
          <span className="text-xs font-black text-amber-300 font-mono tracking-wider drop-shadow-md">
            +{(activeGift.gift.coins * activeGift.comboCount).toLocaleString()} Coins
          </span>
          <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-400 animate-spin" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

// Authentic 21-ray Golden Sun of Kurdistan (Rôj) SVG Emoji
export const KurdistanSunEmoji: React.FC<{ size?: number; className?: string }> = ({
  size = 18,
  className = '',
}) => {
  return (
    <span className={`inline-block align-middle shrink-0 drop-shadow-sm ${className}`} title="ڕۆژی کوردستان ☀️">
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 100 100"
        width={size}
        height={size}
        className="w-full h-full"
        fill="none"
        xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      >
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id="kurdSunCenter" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#fffbeb" />
          <stop offset="40%" stopColor="#fde047" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#eab308" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id="kurdRay" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#facc15" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#ca8a04" />
        </linearGradient>
      </defs>

      {/* 21 Golden Sun Rays of Kurdistan */}
      <g transform="translate(50, 50)">
        {Array.from({ length: 21 }).map((_, i) => {
          const angle = (i * 360) / 21;
          return (
            <polygon
              key={i}
              points="0,-48 4.2,-26 -4.2,-26"
              fill="url(#kurdRay)"
              transform={`rotate(${angle})`}
            />
          );
        })}
        {/* Central Solar Disc */}
        <circle cx="0" cy="0" r="26" fill="url(#kurdSunCenter)" stroke="#d97706" strokeWidth="1.5" />
        <circle cx="0" cy="0" r="21" fill="none" stroke="#fef08a" strokeWidth="1" opacity="0.8" />
      </g>
    </svg>
    </span>
  );
};

};

// User Badges Row (renders Blue Checkmark + Admin badge)
export const UserBadges: React.FC<{
  user: { isVerified?: boolean; isAdmin?: boolean; username?: string };
  size?: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md';
  className?: string;
}> = ({ user, size = 'sm', className = '' }) => {
  const isGeneralManager = user.username === 'sabah_sndi';

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 align-middle shrink-0 ${className}`}>
      {user.isVerified && <BlueVerifiedBadge size={size} />}
      {isGeneralManager ? (
        <KurdistanAdminVerifyBadge size={size === 'md' ? 'md' : 'sm'} />
      ) : user.isAdmin ? (
        <span
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-500/60 text-amber-300 text-[10px] font-bold select-none"
          title="مدير مساعد / Admin"
        >
          <span>👑</span>
          <span>Admin</span>
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </span>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface KurdChatLogoProps {
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';
  className?: string;
  withGlow?: boolean;
}

export const KurdChatLogo: React.FC<KurdChatLogoProps> = ({
  size = 'lg',
  className = '',
  withGlow = false,
}) => {
  const sizeMap = {
    sm: 'w-9 h-9',
    md: 'w-12 h-12',
    lg: 'w-24 h-24',
    xl: 'w-28 h-28',
  };

  const iconSizeMap = {
    sm: 'w-5 h-5',
    md: 'w-7 h-7',
    lg: 'w-13 h-13',
    xl: 'w-16 h-16',
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`relative inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gradient-to-tr from-purple-700 via-purple-600 to-indigo-500 shadow-xl ${
        withGlow ? 'ring-2 ring-purple-400 ring-offset-2 ring-offset-black shadow-purple-900/40' : ''
      } ${sizeMap[size]} ${className}`}
    >
      {/* Speech bubble icon exactly matching video logo */}
      <svg
        viewBox="0 0 100 100"
        fill="currentColor"
        className={`text-white drop-shadow-md ${iconSizeMap[size]}`}
      >
        <path d="M 50 18 C 28 18 12 32 12 50 C 12 59 16 67 23 73 C 22 79 19 86 15 88 C 23 88 32 83 37 80 C 41 81 45 82 50 82 C 72 82 88 68 88 50 C 88 32 72 18 50 18 Z" />
      </svg>
      {/* Kurdish sun sparkle accent */}
      <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 flex h-3 w-3">
        <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-amber-400 opacity-75"></span>
        <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-amber-400"></span>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
};
📄 src/components/PWAInstallButton.tsx 133 lines 
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { usePWAInstall } from '../utils/usePWAInstall';
import { Download, Share2, Smartphone, X, CheckCircle, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

interface PWAInstallButtonProps {
  className?: string;
  variant?: 'banner' | 'button' | 'compact';
}

export const PWAInstallButton: React.FC<PWAInstallButtonProps> = ({
  className = '',
  variant = 'compact',
}) => {
  const { isInstallable, isInstalled, isIOS, install } = usePWAInstall();
  const [showIOSGuide, setShowIOSGuide] = useState(false);
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState(false);

  const handleShareApp = async () => {
    const shareData = {
      title: 'Kurd Chat | کورد چات',
      text: 'وەرە ناو کورد چات، ئەپڵیکەیشنی گفتوگۆی کوردی بە نامەی دەنگی و ستۆری ☀️💬',
      url: window.location.href,
    };

    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share(shareData);
      } catch {}
    } else {
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
        setCopiedLink(true);
        setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 2000);
      } catch {}
    }
  };

  // If already running inside installed standalone PWA, offer the Share link
  if (isInstalled) {
    return (
      <button
        onClick={handleShareApp}
        title="هاوبەشکردنی لینک"
        className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-950/50 hover:bg-purple-900/60 border border-purple-800/50 text-xs text-purple-200 transition-all cursor-pointer ${className}`}
      >
        <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400" />
        <span>{copiedLink ? 'لینک کۆپیکرا ✅' : 'هاوبەشکردنی ئەپ'}</span>
      </button>
    );
  }

  // Android / Chromium direct install
  if (isInstallable) {
    return (
      <button
        onClick={install}
        className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-md shadow-blue-600/30 transition-all cursor-pointer active:scale-95 animate-pulse ${className}`}
      >
        <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>داگرتنی ئەپ (Install)</span>
      </button>
    );
  }

  // iOS Safari flow
  if (isIOS) {
    return (
      <>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowIOSGuide(true)}
          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-neutral-300 transition-all cursor-pointer ${className}`}
        >
          <Smartphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400" />
          <span>داگرتن لەسەر ئایفۆن</span>
        </button>

        {showIOSGuide && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
            <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 p-6 shadow-2xl text-white text-right flex flex-col gap-4" dir="rtl">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-neutral-800 pb-3">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  داگرتنی ئەپ بۆ iPhone / iPad
                </h3>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setShowIOSGuide(false)}
                  className="p-1 rounded-full text-neutral-400 hover:text-white"
                >
                  <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3 text-xs text-neutral-300 leading-relaxed">
                <p className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">1</span>
                  <span>لە وێبگەڕی Safari، کلیک لە دوگمەی <strong>Share (هاوبەشکردن 📤)</strong> بکە.</span>
                </p>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">2</span>
                  <span>بڕۆ خوارەوە و کلیک لەسەر <strong>Add to Home Screen (زیادکردن بۆ پەڕەی سەرەکی ➕)</strong> بکە.</span>
                </p>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shrink-0">3</span>
                  <span>ئێستا Kurd Chat وەک بەرنامەیەکی فەرمی لەسەر شاشەی مۆبایلەکەت ئامادەیە!</span>
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => setShowIOSGuide(false)}
                className="mt-2 w-full py-2.5 bg-neutral-800 hover:bg-neutral-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl"
              >
                تێگەیشتم
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }

  // Fallback: Share / Install instructions button
  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleShareApp}
      title="هاوبەشکردنی ئەپڵیکەیشن"
      className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800 border border-neutral-800 text-xs text-neutral-300 transition-all cursor-pointer ${className}`}
    >
      <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400" />
      <span>{copiedLink ? 'لینک کۆپیکرا ✅' : 'هاوبەشکردنی ئەپ'}</span>
    </button>
  );
};
